### [1] [TADI: Tool-Augmented Drilling Intelligence via Agentic LLM Orchestration over Heterogeneous Wellsite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00060)
*Rong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TADI（工具增强钻井智能）系统，这是一个将钻井作业数据转化为基于证据的分析智能的代理AI系统。该系统在Equinor Volve Field数据集上应用，整合了多种数据源，采用双存储架构，并通过领域专业化工具设计驱动分析质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻井作业产生大量异构数据（日报、实时监测数据、生产记录等），但缺乏有效方法将这些数据转化为可操作的分析智能。现有系统难以跨结构化测量数据和非结构化叙事报告进行多步骤证据收集和交叉引用，因此需要开发一个能够整合多源数据、支持证据驱动分析的智能系统。

Method: TADI采用双存储架构：DuckDB用于结构化查询（12个表、65,447行），ChromaDB用于语义搜索（36,709个嵌入文档）。系统整合了1,759份钻井日报、WITSML实时对象、15,634条生产记录等地层数据。通过大语言模型协调12个领域专业化工具，采用迭代函数调用实现多步骤证据收集。系统形式化为序列工具选择问题，并提出证据 grounding 评分（EGS）作为grounding合规性代理指标。实现为6,084行无框架代码，包含95个自动化测试和130问题压力测试分类法。

Result: 系统成功解析全部1,759个DDR XML文件（零错误），处理三种不兼容的井命名约定。案例研究和定性消融分析表明，领域专业化工具设计（而非单纯的模型规模）是技术操作中分析质量的主要驱动因素。系统实现了结构化钻井测量与日报叙事的交叉引用，支持多步骤证据收集。完整实现可在公开Volve数据和API密钥条件下复现。

Conclusion: TADI证明了领域专业化工具设计在技术操作分析中的重要性，超越了单纯依赖模型规模的方法。系统成功整合多源异构钻井数据，实现了基于证据的分析智能，为钻井作业数据分析提供了可复现、可测试的解决方案。证据 grounding 评分（EGS）为评估代理系统的证据合规性提供了有效度量。

Abstract: We present TADI (Tool-Augmented Drilling Intelligence), an agentic AI system that transforms drilling operational data into evidence-based analytical intelligence. Applied to the Equinor Volve Field dataset, TADI integrates 1,759 daily drilling reports, selected WITSML real-time objects, 15,634 production records, formation tops, and perforations into a dual-store architecture: DuckDB for structured queries over 12 tables with 65,447 rows, and ChromaDB for semantic search over 36,709 embedded documents. Twelve domain-specialized tools, orchestrated by a large language model via iterative function calling, support multi-step evidence gathering that cross-references structured drilling measurements with daily report narratives. The system parses all 1,759 DDR XML files with zero errors, handles three incompatible well naming conventions, and is backed by 95 automated tests plus a 130-question stress-question taxonomy spanning six operational categories. We formalize the agent's behavior as a sequential tool-selection problem and propose the Evidence Grounding Score (EGS) as a simple grounding-compliance proxy based on measurements, attributed DDR quotations, and required answer sections. The complete 6,084-line, framework-free implementation is reproducible given the public Volve download and an API key, and the case studies and qualitative ablation analysis suggest that domain-specialized tool design, rather than model scale alone, is the primary driver of analytical quality in technical operations.

</details>


### [2] [AgentReputation: A Decentralized Agentic AI Reputation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00073)
*Mohd Sameen Chishti,Damilare Peter Oyinloye,Jingyue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgentReputation，一个针对去中心化智能体AI系统的三层声誉框架，解决了现有声誉机制在验证机制、上下文迁移和验证严格度方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前去中心化智能体AI市场缺乏有效的声誉机制，存在三大问题：智能体可针对评估程序进行策略性优化；能力证明难以在不同任务上下文间迁移；验证严格度差异显著。现有的联邦学习、区块链AI平台和LLM安全研究无法协同解决这些问题。

Method: 提出AgentReputation三层声誉框架：(1)分离任务执行、声誉服务和防篡改持久化层；(2)引入与声誉元数据关联的显式验证机制；(3)设计上下文条件声誉卡防止跨领域声誉混淆；(4)构建决策策略引擎支持资源分配、访问控制和自适应验证升级。

Result: 框架成功实现了各层独立演进与优势互补，通过验证机制关联、上下文条件声誉卡和策略引擎，解决了声誉策略性优化、上下文迁移失效和验证标准不一致的核心挑战。

Conclusion: AgentReputation为去中心化智能体AI市场提供了可行的声誉解决方案，未来研究方向包括验证本体开发、验证强度量化、隐私保护证据机制、冷启动声誉引导和对抗操纵防御。

Abstract: Decentralized, agentic AI marketplaces are rapidly emerging to support software engineering tasks such as debugging, patch generation, and security auditing, often operating without centralized oversight. However, existing reputation mechanisms fail in this setting for three fundamental reasons: agents can strategically optimize against evaluation procedures; demonstrated competence does not reliably transfer across heterogeneous task contexts; and verification rigor varies widely, from lightweight automated checks to costly expert review. Current approaches to reputation drawing on federated learning, blockchain-based AI platforms, and large language model safety research are unable to address these challenges in combination. We therefore propose \textbf{AgentReputation}, a decentralized, three-layer reputation framework for agentic AI systems. The framework separates task execution, reputation services, and tamper-proof persistence to both leverage their respective strengths and enable independent evolution. The framework introduces explicit verification regimes linked to agent reputation metadata, as well as context-conditioned reputation cards that prevent reputation conflation across domains and task types. In addition, AgentReputation provides a decision-facing policy engine that supports resource allocation, access control, and adaptive verification escalation based on risk and uncertainty. Building on this framework, we outline several future research directions, including the development of verification ontologies, methods for quantifying verification strength, privacy-preserving evidence mechanisms, cold-start reputation bootstrapping, and defenses against adversarial manipulation.

</details>


### [3] [Causal Foundations of Collective Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00248)
*Frederik Hytting Jørgensen,Sebastian Weichwald,Lewis Hammond*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为视角和因果博弈的框架，用于判定多智能体系统何时可被视为统一的集体智能体，并应用于分析Actor-Critic模型和投票机制中的集体代理程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级AI系统安全面临的一个关键挑战是：多个简单智能体可能无意中形成一个具有独特能力和目标的集体智能体。更广泛地说，确定一组智能体何时可被视为统一的集体智能体，是研究生物和人工系统中交互与激励的基础性问题。

Method: 采用行为视角，当将群体的联合行动视为理性和目标导向能够成功预测其行为时，将集体代理性归因于该群体。使用因果博弈（战略多智能体交互的因果模型）和因果抽象（形式化简单高层模型何时能忠实捕捉复杂低层模型）来形式化这一视角。

Result: 应用该框架解决了Actor-Critic模型中多智能体激励的难题，并对不同投票机制所表现出的集体代理程度进行了定量评估。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在为理解和预测多智能体AI系统中涌现的集体智能体提供理论基础，并为相关理论和实证工作奠定基础，从而实现对集体智能体的控制。

Abstract: A key challenge for the safety of advanced AI systems is the possibility that multiple simpler agents might inadvertently form a collective agent with capabilities and goals distinct from those of any individual. More generally, determining when a group of agents can be viewed as a unified collective agent is a foundational question in the study of interactions and incentives in both biological and artificial systems. We adopt a behavioral perspective in answering this question, ascribing collective agency to a group when viewing the group's joint actions as rational and goal-directed successfully predicts its behavior. We formalize this perspective on collective agency using causal games -- which are causal models of strategic, multi-agent interactions -- and causal abstraction -- which formalizes when a simple, high-level model faithfully captures a more complex, low-level model. We use this framework to solve a puzzle regarding multi-agent incentives in actor-critic models and to make quantitative assessments of the degree of collective agency exhibited by different voting mechanisms. Our framework aims to provide a foundation for theoretical and empirical work to understand, predict, and control emergent collective agents in multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [4] [Minimal, Local, Causal Explanations for Jailbreak Success in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00123)
*Shubham Kumar,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LOCA方法，通过识别最小可解释的中间表示变化集合，为特定越狱攻击的成功提供局部因果解释，能更有效地诱导模型拒绝有害请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对大语言模型越狱攻击脆弱性的深入理解，仅提供全局解释（如降低有害性概念），而不同越狱策略可能通过不同机制成功，同一策略对不同类型有害请求效果也不同，因此需要局部解释来说明特定越狱为何成功。

Method: 提出LOCA（Local, CAusal explanations）方法，通过识别最小可解释的中间表示变化集合，在原本成功的越狱请求上因果性地诱导模型拒绝，从而解释特定越狱成功的具体原因。

Result: 在Gemma和Llama聊天模型上的大规模越狱基准测试中，LOCA平均通过6个可解释变化即可成功诱导拒绝，而先前方法在20次变化后仍经常无法实现拒绝。

Conclusion: LOCA方法为LLMs中越狱成功的机制化局部解释迈出了重要一步，有助于深入理解模型安全漏洞，为未来高风险场景下的模型安全提供指导。

Abstract: Safety trained large language models (LLMs) can often be induced to answer harmful requests through jailbreak prompts. Because we lack a robust understanding of why LLMs are susceptible to jailbreaks, future frontier models operating more autonomously in higher-stakes settings may similarly be vulnerable to such attacks. Prior work has studied jailbreak success by examining the model's intermediate representations, identifying directions in this space that causally encode concepts like harmfulness and refusal. Then, they globally explain all jailbreak attacks as attempting to reduce or strengthen these concepts (e.g., reduce harmfulness). However, different jailbreak strategies may succeed by strengthening or suppressing different intermediate concepts, and the same jailbreak strategy may not work for different harmful request categories (e.g., violence vs. cyberattack); thus, we seek to give a local explanation -- i.e., why did this specific jailbreak succeed? To address this gap, we introduce LOCA, a method that gives Local, CAusal explanations of jailbreak success by identifying a minimal set of interpretable, intermediate representation changes that causally induce model refusal on an otherwise successful jailbreak request. We evaluate LOCA on harmful original-jailbreak pairs from a large jailbreak benchmark across Gemma and Llama chat models, comparing against prior methods adapted to this setting. LOCA can successfully induce refusal by making, on average, six interpretable changes; prior work routinely fails to achieve refusal even after 20 changes. LOCA is a step toward mechanistic, local explanations of jailbreak success in LLMs. Code to be released.

</details>


### [5] [Are Tools All We Need? Unveiling the Tool-Use Tax in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00136)
*Kaituo Zhang,Zhen Xiong,Mingyu Zhong,Zhimeng Jiang,Zhouyuan Yuan,Zhecheng Li,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具增强推理在存在语义干扰时并不总是优于原生思维链，研究揭示了“工具使用税”这一关键权衡，并提出轻量级门控机制G-STEP以部分缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为工具增强推理能提升大语言模型代理的推理能力和可靠性，但作者发现这一共识在语义干扰存在的场景下并不总是成立，旨在探究其背后的原因。

Method: 提出因式分解干预框架，将性能差异分解为提示格式成本、工具调用协议开销以及工具执行的实际收益三个独立因素；在此基础上提出G-STEP轻量级推理时门控机制以缓解协议引发的错误。

Result: 分析揭示了关键权衡：在语义噪声下，工具带来的收益往往无法抵消“工具使用税”，即工具调用协议本身引入的性能下降；G-STEP机制能够实现部分性能恢复，但效果有限。

Conclusion: 仅靠推理时的门控机制难以完全解决问题，实质性改进仍需从增强模型内在推理能力和工具交互能力入手。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has become a popular direction for LLM-based agents, and it is widely assumed to improve reasoning and reliability. However, we demonstrate that this consensus does not always hold: in the presence of semantic distractors, tool-augmented reasoning does not necessarily outperform native CoT. To explain this performance gap, we propose a Factorized Intervention Framework that isolates the cost of prompt formatting, the overhead of the tool-calling protocol, and the actual gain from executing tools. Our analysis reveals a critical tradeoff: under semantic noise, the gains from tools often fail to offset the "tool-use tax", which is the performance degradation introduced by the tool-calling protocol itself. To address this, we introduce G-STEP, a lightweight inference-time gate to mitigate protocol-induced errors. While this yields partial recovery, our findings suggest that more substantial improvements still require strengthening the model's intrinsic reasoning and tool-interaction capabilities.

</details>


### [6] [TUR-DPO: Topology- and Uncertainty-Aware Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00224)
*Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Seyedali Mirjalili,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TUR-DPO，一种结合拓扑和不确定性的DPO改进方法，通过引入推理拓扑和校准的不确定性信号，在保持无RL训练简单性的同时，显著提升了模型在推理任务上的表现，甚至在某些任务上超越PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的直接偏好优化（DPO）方法虽然稳定且无需强化学习，但存在两个主要局限性：1）将偏好视为扁平的“赢家vs输家”信号，忽略了答案的推导过程；2）对噪声或脆弱的偏好数据敏感，尤其是当这些偏好来自不稳定的思维链时。这导致模型难以准确区分高质量推理和表面匹配。

Method: 提出TUR-DPO（Topology- and Uncertainty-aware DPO），该方法：1）诱导轻量级推理拓扑，关注答案的推导过程而非仅仅是结果；2）将语义忠实度、实用性和拓扑质量结合成一个校准的不确定性信号；3）设计一个可学习的小型奖励函数，基于这些信号进行因子分解；4）将不确定性加权引入DPO目标函数，保持无RL特性，仅依赖固定或移动参考策略。

Result: 在7-8B参数的开源模型上，涵盖数学推理、事实问答、摘要生成和有益/无害对话等多个基准测试中，TUR-DPO相比标准DPO显著提升了：1）评判器胜率；2）语义忠实度；3）模型校准度。同时在多模态和长上下文场景中观察到一致的提升。在以推理为核心的任务上，TUR-DPO达到或超越PPO的表现，同时保持了操作简单性。

Conclusion: TUR-DPO成功地在DPO框架中引入了对推理过程的关注，通过不确定性加权机制有效缓解了噪声偏好的影响。该方法在保持训练简单性和避免在线采样的同时，实现了与PPO相当甚至更优的性能，为LLM偏好对齐提供了一个更优的实践方案。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences is commonly done via reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) or, more simply, via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). While DPO is stable and RL-free, it treats preferences as flat winner vs. loser signals and is sensitive to noisy or brittle preferences arising from fragile chains of thought. We propose TUR-DPO, a topology- and uncertainty-aware variant of DPO that rewards how answers are derived, not only what they say, by eliciting lightweight reasoning topologies and combining semantic faithfulness, utility, and topology quality into a calibrated uncertainty signal. A small learnable reward is factorized over these signals and incorporated into an uncertainty-weighted DPO objective that remains RL-free and relies only on a fixed or moving reference policy. Empirically, across open 7-8B models and benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, factual question answering, summarization, and helpful/harmless dialogue, TUR-DPO improves judge win-rates, faithfulness, and calibration relative to DPO while preserving training simplicity and avoiding online rollouts. We further observe consistent gains in multimodal and long-context settings, and show that TUR-DPO matches or exceeds PPO on reasoning-centric tasks while maintaining operational simplicity.

</details>


### [7] [ARMOR 2025: A Military-Aligned Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Safety Beyond Civilian Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00245)
*Sydney Johns,Heng Jin,Chaoyu Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARMOR 2025，一个基于军事核心原则（战争法、交战规则、联合伦理条例）的对齐安全基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在军事决策场景中的合规性，填补了现有安全基准专注于通用社会风险而忽视军事法律伦理规则的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正在被探索用于国防应用，需要可靠且符合法律规范的决策支持。现有的安全基准测试主要关注通用社会风险，未能测试模型是否遵循实际军事行动中适用的法律和伦理规则。军事用途需要能够反映指导真实军事行动的条令标准的评估方法。

Method: 从三大核心军事原则（战争法、交战规则、联合伦理条例）中提取条令文本，并生成保留每条规则原意的多项选择题。基准测试基于OODA（观察-调整-决策-行动）决策框架构建分类体系，形成了包含12个类别的结构化分类法，创建了519个基于条令的提示词，并建立了严格的评估程序。

Result: 该基准测试应用于21个商业大语言模型的评估，结果揭示了在军事应用中安全对齐方面存在关键差距，表明当前模型在遵循军事法律和伦理规则方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: ARMOR 2025基准测试为评估大语言模型在军事场景中的安全对齐提供了系统化的方法，揭示了现有模型在军事合规性方面的显著缺陷，为未来改进军事应用的LLM安全性和合规性提供了重要的评估工具和方向指引。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now being explored for defense applications that require reliable and legally compliant decision support. They also hold significant potential to enhance decision making, coordination, and operational efficiency in military contexts. These uses demand evaluation methods that reflect the doctrinal standards that guide real military operations. Existing safety benchmarks focus on general social risks and do not test whether models follow the legal and ethical rules that govern real military operations. To address this gap, we introduce ARMOR 2025, a military aligned safety benchmark grounded in three core military doctrines the Law of War, the Rules of Engagement, and the Joint Ethics Regulation. We extract doctrinal text from these sources and generate multiple choice questions that preserve the intended meaning of each rule. The benchmark is organized through a taxonomy informed by the Observe Orient Decide Act (OODA) decision making framework. This structure enables systematic testing of accuracy and refusal across military relevant decision types. This benchmark features a structured 12-category taxonomy, 519 doctrinally grounded prompts, and rigorous evaluation procedures applied to 21 commercial LLMs. Evaluation results reveal critical gaps in safety alignment for military applications.

</details>


### [8] [Agentic AI for Trip Planning Optimization Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00276)
*Tiejin Chen,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Kyungtae Han,Hua Wei,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个智能体AI框架和配套数据集(TOP Dataset)，用于解决智能车辆旅行规划中的路线优化问题，在TOP基准测试上达到77.4%的准确率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能车辆旅行规划系统主要关注可行性而非优化，且现有基准测试缺乏真实最优解，无法客观评估优化性能。随着旅行时间、能耗和交通状况等因素直接影响规划质量，迫切需要能够选择最优路线的系统。

Method: 提出了一个智能体AI框架，通过编排智能体协调专门化的智能体（包括交通、充电和兴趣点智能体）实现动态优化；同时构建了Trip-planning Optimization Problems (TOP) Dataset，提供确定性的最优解和类别级任务结构，支持细粒度分析。

Result: 该系统在TOP基准测试上达到77.4%的准确率，显著优于单智能体和基于工作流的多智能体基线方法，验证了编排式智能体推理在旅行规划优化中的重要性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，编排式智能体推理对于实现鲁棒的旅行规划优化至关重要，所提出的框架和数据集为智能车辆旅行规划的优化评估提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Trip planning for intelligent vehicles increasingly requires selecting optimal routes rather than merely producing feasible itineraries, as interacting factors such as travel time, energy consumption, and traffic conditions directly affect plan quality. Yet existing systems are largely designed for feasibility-oriented planning, and current benchmarks provide only reference answers without ground truth, preventing objective evaluation of optimization performance. In our paper, we address these limitations with an agentic AI framework that enables dynamic refinement through an orchestration agent coordinating specialized agents for traffic, charging, and points of interest, and with the Trip-planning Optimization Problems Dataset, which supplies definitive optimal solutions and category-level task structure for fine-grained analysis. Experiments show that our system achieves 77.4\% accuracy on the TOP Benchmark, significantly outperforming single-agent and workflow-based multi-agent baselines, demonstrating the importance of orchestrated agentic reasoning for robust trip planning optimization.

</details>


### [9] [Token Arena: A Continuous Benchmark Unifying Energy and Cognition in AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00300)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TokenArena，一个在端点粒度（provider, model, SKU）持续评估LLM推理性能的基准框架。综合五个维度（输出速度、首字延迟、价格、上下文、质量）生成三个核心指标，发现同一模型在不同端点表现差异巨大，且工作负载感知的定价会显著改变排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要在模型和供应商层级比较AI系统，但实际部署决策是在端点层级（endpoint granularity）进行的。端点包含了具体的量化策略、解码策略、服务区域和服务栈等信息，同一模型在不同端点上的表现可能截然不同。因此需要一种更细粒度的基准测试方法来辅助实际部署决策。

Method: 提出TokenArena框架，在端点粒度上从五个核心维度测量推理性能：输出速度、首字延迟（TTFT）、工作负载混合价格、有效上下文和端点质量。将这五个维度与建模的能耗估计综合为三个核心指标：每正确答案焦耳数（能源效率）、每正确答案美元数（成本效率）和端点保真度（与第一方参考的输出分布相似度）。对覆盖12个模型家族的78个端点进行持续基准测试。

Result: 同一模型在不同端点上的表现差异显著：数学和代码任务平均准确率差异高达12.5分，与第一方的指纹相似度差异高达12分，尾部延迟相差一个数量级，每正确答案焦耳数相差6.2倍。工作负载感知的混合定价会显著改变排名：聊天预设（3:1输入输出比）下排名前10的端点中有7个在检索增强预设（20:1）下跌出前10，而推理预设（1:5）则提升了聊天预设下因价格受罚的前沿闭源模型的排名。

Conclusion: 端点粒度的评估对实际部署决策至关重要，因为它揭示了模型层级比较所忽略的巨大性能差异。TokenArena作为方法论而非单一排名，通过发布完整的数据溯源、局限性说明和开源代码，欢迎外部复现和验证。该框架为AI系统部署提供了更细粒度、更贴合实际的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Public inference benchmarks compare AI systems at the model and provider level, but the unit at which deployment decisions are actually made is the endpoint: the (provider, model, stock-keeping-unit) tuple at which a specific quantization, decoding strategy, region, and serving stack is exposed. We introduce TokenArena, a continuous benchmark that measures inference at endpoint granularity along five core axes (output speed, time to first token, workload-blended price, effective context, and quality on the live endpoint) and synthesizes them, together with a modeled energy estimate, into three headline composites: joules per correct answer, dollars per correct answer, and endpoint fidelity (output-distribution similarity to a first-party reference). The framework's novelty is empirical and methodological. Across 78 endpoints serving 12 model families, the same model on different endpoints differs in mean accuracy by up to 12.5 points on math and code, in fingerprint similarity to first party by up to 12 points, in tail latency by an order of magnitude, and in modeled joules per correct answer by a factor of 6.2. We further show that workload-aware blended pricing reorders the leaderboard substantially: 7 of 10 top-ranked endpoints under the chat preset (3:1 input:output) fall out of the top 10 under the retrieval-augmented preset (20:1), and the reasoning preset (1:5) elevates frontier closed models that the chat preset penalizes on price. We release the framework, schema, probe and eval harness, and a v1.0 leaderboard snapshot under CC BY 4.0. TokenArena is a methodology, not a single ranking; we publish full provenance and limitations and welcome external replication.

</details>


### [10] [AgentFloor: How Far Up the tool use Ladder Can Small Open-Weight Models Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00334)
*Ranit Karmakar,Jayita Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgentFloor基准测试，发现小中型开源模型足以胜任大多数短程结构化工具使用任务，而大型前沿模型仅在长程规划任务上具有优势，为智能体系统的模型选择提供了实用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的智能体系统每个用户请求会产生大量模型调用，其中大多数是简短、结构化且常规的。现有的评估方法无法直接回答一个关键问题：智能体工作流中的哪些部分真正需要大型前沿模型，哪些可以由较小的模型处理？这一路由问题对于优化成本和性能平衡具有重要意义。

Method: 引入了AgentFloor，一个确定性的30任务基准测试，组织为六层能力阶梯，涵盖指令遵循、工具使用、多步协调以及持久约束下的长程规划。研究评估了16个开源权重模型（参数规模从0.27B到32B）以及GPT-5，共进行了16,542次评分运行。

Result: 研究揭示了模型必要性的清晰边界：小中型开源模型已经足以处理主导真实智能体管道的大部分短程、结构化工具使用工作；总体而言，最强的开源模型在基准测试上匹配GPT-5，同时成本更低、运行更快。差距在长程规划任务上最为明显，这类任务需要持续的协调和多步可靠的约束跟踪，前沿模型在此仍保持优势，但双方都未达到强可靠性。该边界不能仅用规模解释：一些失败可通过针对性干预解决，但效果因模型而异而非通用。

Conclusion: 研究提出了智能体系统的实用设计原则：对于广泛的常规动作使用较小的开源模型，为真正需要深度规划和控制的窄类任务保留大型前沿模型。研究发布了基准测试、工具链、扫描配置和完整运行语料库，为智能体系统的模型路由选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Production agentic systems make many model calls per user request, and most of those calls are short, structured, and routine. This raises a practical routing question that existing evaluations do not directly answer: which parts of an agent workflow truly require large frontier intelligence, and which can be handled by smaller models? We introduce AgentFloor, a deterministic 30-task benchmark organized as a six-tier capability ladder, spanning instruction following, tool use, multi-step coordination, and long-horizon planning under persistent constraints. We evaluate 16 open-weight models, from 0.27B to 32B parameters, alongside GPT-5 across 16,542 scored runs. Our results reveal a clear boundary of model necessity. Small and mid-sized open-weight models are already sufficient for much of the short-horizon, structured tool use work that dominates real agent pipelines, and in aggregate, the strongest open-weight model matches GPT-5 on our benchmark while being substantially cheaper and faster to run. The gap appears most clearly on long-horizon planning tasks that require sustained coordination and reliable constraint tracking over many steps, where frontier models still hold an advantage, though neither side reaches strong reliability. We also find that this boundary is not explained by scale alone: some failures respond to targeted interventions, but the effects are model-specific rather than universal. These findings suggest a practical design principle for agentic systems: use smaller open-weight models for the broad base of routine actions, and reserve large frontier models for the narrower class of tasks that truly demand deeper planning and control. We release the benchmark, harness, sweep configurations, and full run corpus.

</details>


### [11] [Physically Native World Models: A Hamiltonian Perspective on Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00412)
*Sen Cui,Jingheng Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿世界模型，通过将观测编码到结构化潜在相空间并利用哈密顿动力学演化，旨在解决现有世界模型在物理可靠性和动作可控性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究主要分为三条路线（2D视频生成、3D场景重建、JEPA类潜在模型），但它们在为具身决策提供物理可靠、动作可控和长期稳定的预测方面仍存在困难。论文认为世界模型的瓶颈不再仅仅是生成逼真的未来，而是这些未来是否具有物理意义并对动作有用。

Method: 提出哈密顿世界模型：将观测编码到结构化潜在相空间，通过带有控制、耗散和残差项的哈密顿动力学演化潜在状态，将预测轨迹解码为未来观测，并利用生成的轨迹进行规划。

Result: 论文讨论了哈密顿结构如何可能提高可解释性、数据效率和长期稳定性，同时也指出了在涉及摩擦、接触、非保守力和可变形物体的真实机器人场景中的实际挑战。

Conclusion: 哈密顿世界模型为世界建模提供了一个物理基础的视角，有潜力改善现有方法的局限性，但在实际应用中仍需解决复杂物理现象带来的挑战。

Abstract: World models have recently re-emerged as a central paradigm for embodied intelligence, robotics, autonomous driving, and model-based reinforcement learning. However, current world model research is often dominated by three partially separated routes: 2D video-generative models that emphasize visual future synthesis, 3D scene-centric models that emphasize spatial reconstruction, and JEPA-like latent models that emphasize abstract predictive representations. While each route has made important progress, they still struggle to provide physically reliable, action-controllable, and long-horizon stable predictions for embodied decision making. In this paper, we argue that the bottleneck of world models is no longer only whether they can generate realistic futures, but whether those futures are physically meaningful and useful for action. We propose \emph{Hamiltonian World Models} as a physically grounded perspective on world modeling. The key idea is to encode observations into a structured latent phase space, evolve the latent state through Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics with control, dissipation, and residual terms, decode the predicted trajectory into future observations, and use the resulting rollouts for planning. We discuss how Hamiltonian structure may improve interpretability, data efficiency, and long-horizon stability, while also noting practical challenges in real-world robotic scenes involving friction, contact, non-conservative forces, and deformable objects.

</details>


### [12] [AEM: Adaptive Entropy Modulation for Multi-Turn Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00425)
*Haotian Zhao,Yuxin Zhang,Songlin Zhou,Stephen S. -T. Yau,Wenyu Zhang,Lun Tian,Tianshu Zhu,Yifeng Huang,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AEM方法，通过在强化学习训练中自适应调节熵动力学来实现无需额外监督的信用分配，在多个基准测试上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体的强化学习训练中，稀疏的、仅基于结果的奖励使得难以对智能体动作轨迹中的各个步骤进行信用分配。常见的解决方案（如过程奖励模型）增加了监督和调优复杂性，且跨任务泛化能力差。

Method: 提出AEM（一种无需监督的信用分配方法），通过自适应调节强化学习训练过程中的熵动力学来实现更有效的探索-利用权衡。将熵分析从token层面提升到响应层面以减少token采样方差，推导出自然梯度下的熵漂移由优势和相对响应惊讶度的乘积内在支配，进而得到一个实用的代理来重塑训练动态，实现从探索到利用的自然过渡。

Result: 在多种基准测试和参数规模从1.5B到32B的模型上进行了广泛实验，证明了AEM的有效性。在极具挑战性的SWE-bench-Verified基准测试上，将其集成到最先进的基线模型中，取得了显著的1.4%的提升。

Conclusion: AEM方法成功实现了无需额外监督的有效信用分配，通过理论分析和实践验证，为大语言模型智能体的强化学习训练提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced the ability of large language model (LLM) agents to interact with environments and solve multi-turn tasks. Yet effective training remains challenging, as sparse, outcome-only rewards make it difficult to assign credit to individual steps in an agent's action trajectory. A common remedy is to introduce dense intermediate supervision, such as process reward models or auxiliary self-supervised signals, but this increases supervision and tuning complexity and often generalizes poorly across tasks and domains. This paper presents AEM, a supervision-free credit assignment method that adaptively modulates entropy dynamics during RL training to achieve a more effective exploration-exploitation trade-off. Theoretically, we elevate entropy analysis from the token level to the response level to reduce token sampling variance and show that entropy drift under natural gradients is intrinsically governed by the product of the advantage and the relative response surprisal. Specifically, we derive a practical proxy to reshape training dynamics, enabling a natural transition from exploration to exploitation. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and models ranging from 1.5B to 32B parameters demonstrate the effectiveness of AEM, including a notable 1.4 percent gain when integrated into a state-of-the-art baseline on the highly challenging SWE-bench-Verified benchmark.

</details>


### [13] [Thinking in Text and Images: Interleaved Vision--Language Reasoning Traces for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00438)
*Jinkun Liu,Haohan Chi,Lingfeng Zhang,Yifan Xie,YuAn Wang,Long Chen,Hangjun Ye,Xiaoshuai Hao,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IVLR框架，通过交替文本子目标和视觉关键帧的显式中间表示（trace），实现了长时域机器人操作任务中逻辑连贯与几何约束的结合，在LIBERO基准上达到95.5%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略存在缺陷：文本链式思维缺乏空间约束，视觉预测缺乏全局语义约束，而潜在状态规划则不透明。长时域机器人操作需要既逻辑连贯又几何落地的规划。

Method: 提出交错视觉-语言推理（IVLR）框架，核心是显式中间表示trace，交替包含文本子目标和视觉关键帧。测试时，单一原生多模态Transformer自生成全局语义-几何trace，缓存后条件化闭环动作解码器。由于标准数据集缺乏此类标注，通过时序分割演示和使用视觉-语言模型字幕生成构建伪监督。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到95.5%平均成功率（LIBERO-Long为92.4%），在SimplerEnv-WidowX上达到59.4%总成功率。消融实验表明双模态必要性：无trace时LIBERO-Long降至37.7%，仅文本和仅视觉trace分别为62.0%和68.4%，完整交错trace达92.4%。

Conclusion: 交错的视觉-语言trace能有效结合逻辑连贯性与几何约束，对局部损坏和中等执行漂移具有鲁棒性，但在过时或错误的全局计划下仍受限制。该方法为长时域机器人操作提供了有效的规划表示范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires plans that are both logically coherent and geometrically grounded. Existing Vision-Language-Action policies usually hide planning in latent states or expose only one modality: text-only chain-of-thought encodes causal order but misses spatial constraints, while visual prediction provides geometric cues but often remains local and semantically underconstrained. We introduce Interleaved Vision--Language Reasoning (IVLR), a policy framework built around \trace{}, an explicit intermediate representation that alternates textual subgoals with visual keyframes over the full task horizon. At test time, a single native multimodal transformer self-generates this global semantic-geometric trace from the initial observation and instruction, caches it, and conditions a closed-loop action decoder on the trace, original instruction, and current observation. Because standard robot datasets lack such traces, we construct pseudo-supervision by temporally segmenting demonstrations and captioning each stage with a vision-language model. Across simulated benchmarks for long-horizon manipulation and visual distribution shift, \method{} reaches 95.5\% average success on LIBERO, including 92.4\% on LIBERO-Long, and 59.4\% overall success on SimplerEnv-WidowX. Ablations show that both modalities are necessary: without traces, LIBERO-Long success drops to 37.7\%; text-only and vision-only traces reach 62.0\% and 68.4\%, while the full interleaved trace reaches 92.4\%. Stress tests with execution perturbations and masked trace content show moderate degradation, suggesting that the trace can tolerate local corruption and moderate execution drift, but remains limited under stale or incorrect global plans.

</details>


### [14] [On the Role of Artificial Intelligence in Human-Machine Symbiosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00440)
*Ching-Chun Chang,Yuchen Guo,Hanrui Wang,Timo Spinde,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推断并嵌入AI功能角色的方法，能够仅从生成文本中恢复AI参与的性质，有效区分辅助编辑与创意生成两种角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的发展，人机界限日益模糊，AI生成信息往往源于人机共同塑造，难以判断AI是否参与。核心问题从“AI是否参与”转变为“AI如何参与”。通常AI的角色由输入提示指定，但脱离对话上下文后难以追溯，因此需要一种方法来追踪AI在自然语言生成中的功能角色。

Method: 提出一种方法论，首先推断由提示指定的潜在角色，然后将该角色嵌入到概率生成过程中的内容里，最终仅从生成的文本中恢复AI参与的性质。实验设定AI扮演辅助代理（编辑人类撰写的内容）或创意代理（根据简短概念生成新内容）两种代表性角色。

Result: 实验结果支持该方法的有效性，能够在角色区分、抗扰动鲁棒性以及语言质量保持方面表现良好，成功从生成文本中恢复AI的功能角色。

Conclusion: 本研究为未来关于AI伦理（公平、透明、适当地使用AI）的研究提供了贡献，有助于判断AI是否被公平、透明和适当地使用。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence (AI) has rendered the boundary between humanity and computational machinery increasingly ambiguous. In the presence of more interwoven relationships within human-machine symbiosis, the very notion of AI-generated information becomes difficult to define, as such information arises not from either humans or machines in isolation, but from their mutual shaping. Therefore, a more pertinent question lies not merely in whether AI has participated, but in how it has participated. In general, the role assumed by AI is often specified, either implicitly or explicitly, in the input prompt, yet becomes less apparent or altogether unobservable when the generated content alone is available. Once detached from the dialogue context, the functional role may no longer be traceable. This study considers the problem of tracing the functional role played by AI in natural language generation. A methodology is proposed to infer the latent role specified by the prompt, embed this role into the content during the probabilistic generation process and subsequently recover the nature of AI participation from the resulting text. Experimentation is conducted under a representative scenario in which AI acts either as an assistive agent that edits human-written content or as a creative agent that generates new content from a brief concept. The experimental results support the validity of the proposed methodology in terms of discrimination between roles, robustness against perturbations and preservation of linguistic quality. We envision that this study may contribute to future research on the ethics of AI with regard to whether AI has been used fairly, transparently and appropriately.

</details>


### [15] [Instance-Aware Parameter Configuration in Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing for the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00572)
*Yinghao Qin,Xinwei Wang,Mosab Bazargani,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实例感知的参数配置方法，通过回归模型预测未见实例的最优参数，解决了组合优化中全局参数配置无法适应实例异质性的问题，在电动车辆路径规划问题上实现了目标值平均降低0.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化算法的性能对参数设置高度敏感，单一的全局调优配置往往无法充分利用实例的异质性。这一局限性在电动带容量约束车辆路径问题（E-CVRP）中尤为明显，因为不同实例在结构、需求模式和能量约束上存在显著差异。

Method: 针对双层迟接受爬山算法（Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing），本文提出实例感知的参数配置方法：首先利用离线调优过程获取实例特定的参数标签，然后建立从实例特征到参数的回归模型，从而在执行前为未见实例预测最优参数配置。

Result: 在IEEE WCCI 2020基准数据集及其扩展上的实验表明，与全局调优配置相比，所提方法在8个留出测试实例上实现了平均0.28%的目标值降低，这在数百万美元规模的运输运营中对应显著的成本节约。

Conclusion: 实例感知的参数配置方法能够有效利用实例异质性，提升算法性能，相比传统全局参数配置具有明显优势，对大规模运输运营具有重要的实际价值。

Abstract: Algorithm performance in combinatorial optimization is highly sensitive to parameter settings, while a single globally tuned configuration often fails to exploit the heterogeneity of instances. This limitation is particularly evident in the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem, where instances differ in structure, demand patterns, and energy constraints. This paper investigates instance-aware parameter configuration for Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing, a state-of-the-art metaheuristic for the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem. An offline tuning procedure is used to obtain instance-specific parameter labels, which are then mapped from instance features via a regression model to enable parameter prediction for unseen instances prior to execution. Experimental results on the IEEE WCCI 2020 benchmark and its extensions show that the proposed approach achieves an average objective value reduction of $0.28\%$ across eight held-out test instances relative to a globally tuned configuration. This corresponds to a significant cost reduction in multimillion-dollar transportation operations.

</details>


### [16] [Learn where to Click from Yourself: On-Policy Self-Distillation for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00642)
*Yan Zhang,Daiqing Wu,Huawen Shen,Yu Zhou,Can Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GUI-SD，首个专门针对GUI定位任务的策略自蒸馏框架，通过构建视觉增强的特权上下文和熵引导蒸馏策略，在准确率和训练效率上均优于基于GRPO的强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法（如GRPO）在GUI定位任务中虽表现优异，但依赖昂贵的多次rollout且在困难样本上信号稀疏。策略自蒸馏（OPSD）可从单次rollout提供密集的token级监督，是一种有潜力的替代方案，但其在GUI定位领域的适用性尚未被探索。

Method: 提出GUI-SD框架，包含两大核心组件：（1）为目标边界框和高斯软掩码构建视觉增强的特权上下文，为教师模型提供信息丰富的指导而不泄露精确坐标；（2）采用熵引导蒸馏策略，根据数字重要性和教师置信度自适应加权token，将优化集中于最具影响力和可靠的位置。

Result: 在六个代表性GUI定位基准上的大量实验表明，GUI-SD在准确率和训练效率上均持续优于基于GRPO的方法和朴素OPSD方法。

Conclusion: GUI-SD成功将策略自蒸馏应用于GUI定位任务，有效解决了强化学习方法计算成本高和信号稀疏的问题，为GUI定位提供了一种更高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding maps natural language instructions to the visual coordinates of target elements and serves as a core capability for autonomous GUI agents. Recent reinforcement learning methods (e.g., GRPO) have achieved strong performance, but they rely on expensive multiple rollouts and suffer from sparse signals on hard samples. These limitations make on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), which provides dense token-level supervision from a single rollout, a promising alternative. However, its applicability to GUI grounding remains unexplored. In this paper, we present GUI-SD, the first OPSD framework tailored for GUI grounding. First, it constructs a visually enriched privileged context for the teacher using a target bounding box and a Gaussian soft mask, providing informative guidance without leaking exact coordinates. Second, it employs entropy-guided distillation, which adaptively weights tokens based on digit significance and teacher confidence, concentrating optimization on the most impactful and reliable positions. Extensive experiments on six representative GUI grounding benchmarks show that GUI-SD consistently outperforms GRPO-based methods and naive OPSD in both accuracy and training efficiency. Code and training data are available at https://zhangyan-ucas.github.io/GUI-SD/.

</details>


### [17] [To Call or Not to Call: A Framework to Assess and Optimize LLM Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00737)
*Qinyuan Wu,Soumi Das,Mahsa Amani,Arijit Nag,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于决策理论的框架来评估LLM的Web搜索工具调用决策，发现模型感知的工具需求与效用常与真实情况错位，通过训练轻量级估计器改进决策质量，在三个任务和六个模型上取得了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的代理型AI架构通过为LLM配备外部工具来增强能力，但工具调用并不总是有益的，某些调用可能是冗余甚至有害的。因此，有效的工具使用取决于LLM的核心决策：在执行任务时是否调用工具。这一决策对于Web搜索工具尤其具有挑战性，因为外部信息的收益取决于模型的内部知识及其整合潜在噪声工具响应的能力。

Method: 作者引入了一个受决策理论启发的原则性框架，从三个关键因素评估Web搜索工具使用决策：必要性、效用和可负担性。分析结合了两个互补视角：规范性视角（从工具调用的最优分配推断真实需求和效用）和描述性视角（从模型观察到的行为推断其自我感知的需求和效用）。基于此框架，训练了基于模型隐藏状态的轻量级需求和效用估计器，构建简单控制器来改进决策。

Result: 研究发现模型的感知工具调用需求和效用经常与真实需求和效用不一致。基于隐藏状态训练的轻量级估计器所构建的控制器能够改进决策质量，在三个任务和六个模型上取得了比自我感知设置更强的任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过规范化工具调用决策评估框架并利用模型隐藏状态估计真实需求与效用，可以有效识别和纠正模型感知与实际情况的错位，从而提升工具使用的决策质量和整体任务性能。

Abstract: Agentic AI architectures augment LLMs with external tools, unlocking strong capabilities. However, tool use is not always beneficial; some calls may be redundant or even harmful. Effective tool use, therefore, hinges on a core LLM decision: whether to call or not call a tool, when performing a task. This decision is particularly challenging for web search tools, where the benefits of external information depend on the model's internal knowledge and its ability to integrate potentially noisy tool responses. We introduce a principled framework inspired by decision-making theory to evaluate web search tool-use decisions along three key factors: necessity, utility, and affordability. Our analysis combines two complementary lenses: a normative perspective that infers true need and utility from an optimal allocation of tool calls, and a descriptive perspective that infers the model's self-perceived need and utility from their observed behaviors. We find that models' perceived need and utility of tool calls are often misaligned with their true need and utility. Building on this framework, we train lightweight estimators of need and utility based on models' hidden states. Our estimators enable simple controllers that can improve decision quality and lead to stronger task performance than the self-perceived set up across three tasks and six models.

</details>


### [18] [Position: agentic AI orchestration should be Bayes-consistent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00742)
*Theodore Papamarkou,Pierre Alquier,Matthias Bauer,Wray Buntine,Andrew Davison,Gintare Karolina Dziugaite,Maurizio Filippone,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Vincent Fortuin,Dimitris Fouskakis,Jes Frellsen,Eyke Hüllermeier,Theofanis Karaletsos,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Nikita Kotelevskii,Salem Lahlou,Yingzhen Li,Fang Liu,Clare Lyle,Thomas Möllenhoff,Konstantina Palla,Maxim Panov,Yusuf Sale,Kajetan Schweighofer,Artem Shelmanov,Siddharth Swaroop,Martin Trapp,Willem Waegeman,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在智能体AI系统的控制层（编排层）应用贝叶斯决策理论，而非直接改造LLM本身，以实现不确定性下的最优决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在预测与推理任务上表现出色，但在高价值部署场景中面临不确定性决策问题（如工具调用选择、专家咨询、资源分配），而将LLM本身改造为显式贝叶斯推断引擎计算开销大且概念复杂。

Method: 提出在智能体AI系统的控制层（编排层）引入贝叶斯决策理论框架，维护任务相关隐变量的信念分布，通过观测到的智能体交互与人机交互更新信念，并基于效用感知策略选择行动。

Result: 提炼出适合现代智能体AI系统与人机协作的贝叶斯控制特性，并通过具体示例与设计模式展示校准信念与效用感知策略如何改进智能体编排。

Conclusion: 连贯的决策制定要求在智能体系统的编排层级引入贝叶斯原则，而非必须在LLM参数层面实现，这为智能体AI编排提供了清晰的理论框架与实践指导。

Abstract: LLMs excel at predictive tasks and complex reasoning tasks, but many high-value deployments rely on decisions under uncertainty, for example, which tool to call, which expert to consult, or how many resources to invest. While the usefulness and feasibility of Bayesian approaches remain unclear for LLM inference, this position paper argues that the control layer of an agentic AI system (that orchestrates LLMs and tools) is a clear case where Bayesian principles should shine. Bayesian decision theory provides a framework for agentic systems that can help to maintain beliefs over task-relevant latent quantities, to update these beliefs from observed agentic and human-AI interactions, and to choose actions. Making LLMs themselves explicitly Bayesian belief-updating engines remains computationally intensive and conceptually nontrivial as a general modeling target. In contrast, this paper argues that coherent decision-making requires Bayesian principles at the orchestration level of the agentic system, not necessarily the LLM agent parameters. This paper articulates practical properties for Bayesian control that fit modern agentic AI systems and human-AI collaboration, and provides concrete examples and design patterns to illustrate how calibrated beliefs and utility-aware policies can improve agentic AI orchestration.

</details>

*Aurélien Bück-Kaeffer,Sneheel Sarangi,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Reihaneh Rabbany,Zachary Yang,Jean-François Godbout*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文对模拟社会网络中的关键设计选择进行了系统分析，发现基础LLM的选择是影响仿真结果的最重要变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着利用大语言模型模拟人类社会行为的研究增多，以及非受控环境下纯LLM社交网络的出现，这些网络的设计空间仍缺乏深入研究，导致模型真实性验证存在空白，需要指导未来研究做出更明智的设计决策。

Method: 系统分析模拟社会网络中关键设计选择的影响与相互作用，包括用于建模个体代理的基础模型选择以及代理间的连接方式，并使用调查问卷作为代理意见的代理指标。

Result: 设计空间的几何特性是非平凡的，部分参数呈加性作用，而其他参数则表现出更复杂的交互作用；基础LLM的选择是影响仿真结果最重要的变量。

Conclusion: 基础模型的选择对模拟社会网络的输出结果具有决定性影响，未来研究在设计此类仿真时应重点关注模型选择及其他设计参数的复杂交互效应。

Abstract: Studies attempting to simulate human behavior with $\textit{Silicon Societies}$ grow in numbers while LLM-only social networks have started appearing outside of controlled settings. However, the design space of these networks remains under-studied, which contributes to a gap in validating model realism. To enable future works to make more informed design decisions, we perform a systematic analysis of the consequences and interactions of key design choices in simulated social networks, including the choice of base model used to model individual agents, and how they are connected to each other. Using surveys as a proxy for agent opinions, our findings suggest that the geometry of the design space is non-trivial, with some parameters behaving in additive ways while others display more complex interactions. In particular, the choice of the base LLM is the most important variable impacting the simulation outcomes.

</details>


### [20] [Foresight Arena: An On-Chain Benchmark for Evaluating AI Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00420)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Foresight Arena，首个基于区块链的AI预测能力评估平台，通过合规评分规则隔离预测能力，经实证分析发现检测α*=0.02的预测优势需要约350次预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI预测能力评估基准存在关键缺陷：依赖静态数据集易受训练数据污染，或使用交易盈亏（PnL）作为指标，但该指标混淆了预测准确性与时机选择、仓位规模和风险偏好。评估真实预测能力需要抗过拟合、去信任化且激励相容的环境。

Method: 构建Foresight Arena平台：AI代理通过Solidity智能合约在Polygon PoS上以提交-揭示协议对Polymarket二元市场提交概率预测；使用Gnosis条件代币框架进行无信任结果解析。采用Brier Score和新颖的Alpha Score作为合规评分规则，激励诚实概率报告并隔离相对市场共识的预测优势。提供形式化分析：Alpha的闭式方差、与Murphy Brier分解的联系，以及区分不同技能水平代理所需轮数的功效分析。

Result: 在80%统计功效下，检测α*=0.02的真实优势需要约350个已解析二元预测（50轮×7市场），α*=0.01需要四倍样本量。对五个前沿LLM代理进行的50轮实时评估显示，Murphy分解能有效区分校准良好的代理与因分辨率降低而失败的市场追踪型代理。

Conclusion: Foresight Arena建立了AI预测能力的可信评估范式：通过链上基础设施消除中心化信任依赖，通过合规评分规则隔离纯粹预测能力，通过形式化功效分析确保统计可靠性，为AI预测系统提供了首个激励相容的评估基准。

Abstract: Evaluating the true forecasting ability of AI agents requires environments resistant to overfitting, free from centralized trust, and grounded in incentive-compatible scoring. Existing benchmarks either rely on static datasets vulnerable to training-data contamination, or measure trading PnL -- a metric conflating predictive accuracy with timing, sizing, and risk appetite. We introduce Foresight Arena, the first permissionless, on-chain benchmark for evaluating AI forecasting agents on real-world prediction markets. Agents submit probabilistic forecasts on binary Polymarket markets via a commit-reveal protocol enforced by Solidity smart contracts on Polygon PoS; outcomes are resolved trustlessly through the Gnosis Conditional Token Framework. Performance is measured by the Brier Score and a novel Alpha Score -- proper scoring rules that incentivize honest probability reporting and isolate predictive edge over market consensus. We provide a formal analysis: closed-form variance for per-market Alpha, the connection to Murphy's classical Brier decomposition, and a power analysis characterizing the number of rounds required to reliably distinguish agents of different skill levels. We show that detecting a true edge of $α^* = 0.02$ at 80% power requires approximately 350 resolved binary predictions (50 rounds of 7 markets), while $α^* = 0.01$ requires four times more. We complement these analytical results with a 50-round live evaluation of five frontier LLM agents plus a random baseline. Murphy decomposition distinguishes well-calibrated agents from market-tracking agents that fail through reduced resolution. All smart contracts and evaluation infrastructure are open-source.

</details>


### [21] [Learning to Act and Cooperate for Distributed Black-Box Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00691)
*Zi-Bo Qin,Feng-Feng Wei,Tai-You Chen,Wei-Neng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了LAC-MAS框架，利用大语言模型从历史轨迹中学习，自适应地设计智能体的动作行为和协作模式，从而解决了分布式黑盒共识优化中人工规则难以平衡探索与收敛的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式黑盒共识优化方法主要依赖人工设计的更新规则和静态协作模式，在异构非凸环境中难以有效平衡局部适应、全局协调与通信效率。

Method: 首先重新设计了具有自适应内部机制的智能体级群体动力学；在此基础上提出了LAC-MAS框架，利用大语言模型从历史优化轨迹中提供稀疏的高层指导，以塑造智能体内部动作行为和外部协作模式；引入了分阶段认知调度策略以资源感知的方式激活不同形式的自适应机制。

Result: 在标准分布式黑盒基准测试和真实世界分布式任务上的实验表明，LAC-MAS在解的质量、收敛效率和通信效率方面均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为从人工设计的分布式协调向自设计多智能体优化系统的转变提供了一条实用路径，展示了轨迹驱动自设计方法在分布式优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Distributed blackbox consensus optimization is a fundamental problem in multi-agent systems, where agents must improve a global objective using only local objective queries and limited neighbor communication. Existing methods largely rely on handcrafted update rules and static cooperation patterns, which often struggle to balance local adaptation, global coordination, and communication efficiency in heterogeneous nonconvex environments. In this paper, we take an initial step toward trajectory-driven self-design for distributed black-box consensus optimization. We first redesign the agent-level swarm dynamics with an adaptive internal mechanism tailored to decentralized consensus settings, improving the balance between exploration, convergence, and local escape. Built on top of this adaptive execution layer, we propose Learning to Act and Cooperate (LACMAS), a trajectorydriven framework in which large language models provide sparse highlevel guidance for shaping both agentinternal action behaviors and agentexternal cooperation patterns from historical optimization trajectories. We further introduce a phased cognitive scheduling strategy to activate different forms of adaptation in a resource-aware manner. Experiments on standard distributed black-box benchmarks and real-world distributed tasks show that LAC-MAS consistently improves solution quality, convergence efficiency, and communication efficiency over strong baselines, suggesting a practical route from handcrafted distributed coordination toward self-designing multi-agent optimization systems.

</details>

### [22] [DeGenTWeb: A First Look at LLM-dominant Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00087)
*Sichang Steven He,Calvin Ardi,Ramesh Govindan,Harsha V. Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeGenTWeb系统，用于系统性识别网络上由大语言模型（LLM）主导生成的网站，发现此类网站在Common Crawl和Bing搜索结果中广泛存在且呈增长趋势，同时指出针对最新LLM的准确识别仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期新闻常声称LLM生成内容正在占据网络，但这些主张通常缺乏代表性样本，方法论不透明，且在降低误报率时，现有LLM文本检测器的表现远低于宣传效果。因此，人们对网络上LLM内容的真实普及程度和特征缺乏了解。

Method: 提出DeGenTWeb系统，系统性识别“LLM主导网站”（即内容主要由LLM生成、人工参与极少的站点）。方法包括：将LLM文本检测器适配至网页内容检测，并将同一站点多个页面的检测结果聚合，以实现准确的站点级分类。

Result: 利用DeGenTWeb发现，LLM主导网站在Common Crawl数据和Bing搜索结果中均高度普遍，且其份额随时间增长。同时，研究表明，考虑到最新LLM的能力，持续准确识别此类网站仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 网络上LLM生成内容的实际规模被证实并持续扩大，但现有检测手段在面对先进LLM时效果有限，未来需提升检测技术以应对日益复杂的生成内容。

Abstract: Many recent news reports have claimed that content generated by large language models (LLMs) is taking over the web. However, these claims are typically not based on a representative sample of the web and the methodology underlying them is often opaque. Moreover, when aiming to minimize the chances of falsely attributing human-authored content to LLMs, we find that detectors of LLM-generated text perform much worse than advertised. Consequently, we lack an understanding of the true prevalence and characteristics of LLM content on the web.
  We describe DeGenTWeb which systematically identifies LLM-dominant websites: sites whose content has been generated using LLMs with little human input. We show how to adapt detectors of LLM-generated text for use on web pages, and how to aggregate detection results from multiple pages on a site for accurate site-level categorization. Using DeGenTWeb, we find that LLM-dominant sites are highly prevalent both in data from Common Crawl and in Bing's search results, and that this share is growing over time. We also show that continuing to accurately identify such sites appears challenging given the capabilities of the latest LLMs.

</details>


### [23] [Network Digital Untwinning: Towards Backward Optimization of Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00169)
*Zifan Zhang,Dianwei Chen,Anjun Gao,Manhua Wang,Mingzhe Chen,Minghong Fang,Xianfeng Yang,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了网络数字反孪生框架，通过SRU和PRU机制实现NDT的选择性移除，在保证模型完整性的同时满足合规和隐私需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络数字孪生（NDT）依赖多样化和敏感数据，在设备停用、网络重配置或法规合规等场景下需要选择性移除数据，但传统方法无法有效保持孪生模型的完整性。

Method: 提出包含两个互补组件的框架：单请求反孪生（SRU）利用地理邻近性、数据分布和网络属性的连接性度量，通过最优回滚检查点、注入高斯噪声和精确重映射来移除目标NDT及其传播影响；并行请求反孪生（PRU）通过聚类相似属性的NDT，协调处理多个移除请求。

Result: 提供了模型与从头构建的孪生在不可区分性方面的理论保证，并在真实流量数据上进行了广泛实验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效实现NDT的选择性移除，同时保持模型完整性，证明了其在实际网络管理中的有效性和操作效率。

Abstract: Network digital twins (NDTs) are transforming network management by offering precise virtual replicas of physical network systems. However, their reliance on diverse and sensitive data introduces significant challenges related to data management, regulatory compliance, and user privacy. In scenarios where selective data removal is necessary, such as device deactivation, network reconfiguration, or regulatory compliance, traditional approaches often fall short of preserving the integrity of the twin model. To address this gap, we introduce a network digital untwinning framework that enables the targeted removal of deprecated NDT contributions while maintaining model integrity. Our approach comprises two complementary components: Single Request Untwinning (\algO) and Parallel Request Untwinning (\algM) mechanisms. \algO leverages connectivity metrics based on geographical proximity, data distribution, and network-level attributes to identify and remove the target NDT along with its propagating influence. This is achieved through an optimally selected rollback checkpoint augmented with injected Gaussian noise, followed by a precise remapping phase. \algM extends this mechanism to efficiently handle multiple removal requests by clustering NDTs with similar attributes and performing a coordinated rollback and untwinning schedule. We provide theoretical guarantees on model indistinguishability from scratch-built twins, and validate the framework through extensive experiments on real-world traffic data, demonstrating its effectiveness and operational efficiency.

</details>


### [24] [RouteProfile: Elucidating the Design Space of LLM Profiles for Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00180)
*Jingjun Xu,Hongji Pu,Tao Feng,Haozhen Zhang,Jiaxuan You,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteProfile框架，系统研究了LLM画像设计对路由性能的影响。发现结构化画像优于扁平画像，查询级信号比领域级信号更可靠，且结构化画像在新模型泛化中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生态系统的扩展，不同模型在各查询、基准和领域上表现出不同能力，推动了LLM路由的发展。然而现有工作主要关注路由机制设计，而捕获模型能力的LLM画像设计仍未被充分探索。需要明确画像在路由中的作用，将画像设计与路由设计解耦，并为路由系统的公平比较和有原则开发提供基础。

Method: 将LLM画像视为异构交互历史上的结构化信息集成问题。开发了LLM画像的通用设计空间RouteProfile，包含四个关键维度：组织形式、表示类型、聚合深度和学习配置。通过在三个代表性路由器上进行系统评估，涵盖标准设置和新LLM泛化设置。

Result: （1）结构化画像始终优于扁平画像；（2）查询级信号比粗粒度领域级信号更可靠；（3）在可训练配置下，结构化画像对新引入模型的泛化效果最好。

Conclusion: LLM画像设计是未来路由研究的重要方向，应受到更多关注和系统性研究。

Abstract: As the large language model (LLM) ecosystem expands, individual models exhibit varying capabilities across queries, benchmarks, and domains, motivating the development of LLM routing. While prior work has largely focused on router mechanism design, LLM profiles, which capture model capabilities, remain underexplored. In this work, we ask: How does LLM profile design affect routing performance across different routers? Addressing this question helps clarify the role of profiles in routing, disentangle profile design from router design, and enable fairer comparison and more principled development of routing systems. To this end, we view LLM profiling as a structured information integration problem over heterogeneous interaction histories. We develop a general design space of LLM profiles, named RouteProfile, along four key dimensions: organizational form, representation type, aggregation depth, and learning configuration. Through systematic evaluation across three representative routers under both standard and new-LLM generalization settings, we show that: (1) structured profiles consistently outperform flat ones; (2) query-level signals are more reliable than coarse domain-level signals; and (3) generalization to newly introduced models benefits most from structured profiles under trainable configurations. Overall, our work highlights LLM profile design as an important direction for future routing research.

</details>


### [25] [A Multi-Perspective Study of the Internet Shutdown in Iran](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00187)
*Ali Sadeghi Jahromi,Jason Jaskolka*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了伊朗2026年两次全国性互联网关闭事件，发现伊朗从2019年的部分BGP撤回转变为2022年后的纯空路由阻断，通过控制平面与转发平面解耦来规避BGP监测，实现对96.5%-97.4%前缀的集中式阻断，同时学术网络和ArvanCloud CDN获得豁免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊朗在2026年1月和3月实施了两次全国性互联网关闭，其中后者是记录以来持续时间最长的伊朗网络中断事件。研究旨在理解伊朗互联网关闭的实施机制、演变过程及其对网络监测的影响，特别是为何BGP监测工具无法检测到这些关闭事件。

Method: 采用三平面方法论：(1)被动Censys扫描数据分析；(2)从五个观测点进行主动TCP可达性探测；(3)对2019至2026年间33个RIPE RIS快照进行BGP分析。研究覆盖4,571个BGP可见的伊朗前缀，结合被动扫描和主动探测技术。

Result: 伊朗通过集中化边境的转发平面空路由实施关闭，BGP公告保持稳定；96.5%-97.4%的伊朗前缀在所有观测点被空路由。被动扫描显示两次关闭间可见主机增加3.7倍（测量伪影非恢复）。发现两项结构性豁免：学术网络从1.4%增至66.6%可见主机占比；ArvanCloud CDN保持99.7%可见性，而其他主要运营商下降至少77%。伊朗从2019年部分BGP撤回演变为2022年后的纯空路由策略。

Conclusion: 伊朗采用的空路由机制实现了控制平面与转发平面的解耦，使基于BGP的断网监测工具无法检测到关闭事件。这种集中协调的阻断机制允许选择性豁免特定网络（学术网络和CDN），反映了国家层面的精细化管理能力。该发现对互联网自由监测和关闭检测方法具有重要启示，表明需要开发超越BGP分析的新监测技术。

Abstract: Iran conducted two nationwide Internet shutdowns in January and March 2026, the latter ongoing at the time of writing and the longest documented Iranian disruption. Using a three-plane methodology combining passive Censys scan data, active TCP reachability probing from five vantage points, and BGP analysis across 33 RIPE RIS snapshots from 2019 to 2026, we show that the 2022 and 2026 shutdowns are enforced via forwarding-plane null-routing at a centralized border while BGP announcements remain stable, and that Iran shifted from partial BGP withdrawal in 2019 to pure null-routing by 2022. This control- and forwarding-plane decoupling prevents BGP-based outage monitors from detecting shutdowns.
  Active probing of 4,571 BGP-visible Iranian prefixes shows that 96.5 to 97.4% are null-routed across all vantage points, indicating a centrally coordinated mechanism. Passive scan analysis reveals a 3.7 times increase in visible hosts between shutdown events due to measurement artifacts rather than recovery, along with two structural exemptions: academic networks rise from 1.4 to 66.6% of visible hosts during partial recovery, and ArvanCloud CDN retains 99.7% visibility while other major operators drop by at least 77%.

</details>


### [26] [Fidelity-Guaranteed Entanglement Routing with Distributed Purification Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00246)
*Anthony Gatti,Anoosha Fayyaz,Prashant Krishnamurthy,Kaushik P. Seshadreesan,Amy Babay*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Q-GUARD，一种分布式的量子纠缠路由算法，能够在仅与$k$跳邻居交换链路状态信息的分布式协议模型下，保证端到端贝尔态保真度满足请求特定的阈值要求，显著提升了量子网络的合格服务范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子纠缠路由算法存在两难困境：要么仅优化吞吐量而忽略保真度，要么需要集中式控制器与全局链路状态知识来保证保真度。而许多量子网络应用需要端到端贝尔态的保真度超过特定阈值，因此迫切需要一种能够在分布式协议模型下强制执行每请求保真度阈值要求的算法。

Method: Q-GUARD在每轮链路结果实现后执行三步：1) 根据已实现的贝尔态对构建每链路纯化代价表；2) 利用Werner态均分规则分配逐跳保真度目标；3) 使用分段局部期望有效吞吐量(EXG)指标选择候选路径段，该指标联合考虑交换成功率、纯化开销和资源可用性。此外，Q-GUARD-WS扩展利用每链路硬件质量估计，在跳间非均匀分配纯化工作。

Result: 在具有异构链路保真度的合成100节点拓扑和随机BBPSSW纯化场景下，Q-GUARD将4跳路径的合格成功率从不到20%提升至超过85%，与仅优化吞吐量和朴素纯化基线相比，合格服务半径在欧几里得距离上几乎翻倍；Q-GUARD-WS在高硬件异构性下提供了额外的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: Q-GUARD成功实现了分布式量子纠缠路由中的保真度保证，克服了现有方法需要全局知识的限制，显著扩展了量子网络的合格服务范围，为量子网络应用提供了实用化的路由解决方案。

Abstract: Many quantum-network applications require end-to-end Bell pairs whose fidelity exceeds a request-specific threshold, but existing entanglement routing algorithms either optimize only throughput without regard for fidelity or enforce fidelity guarantees using centralized controllers with global link-state knowledge. We present Q-GUARD, an online entanglement routing algorithm that enforces per-request fidelity thresholds within a distributed protocol model in which nodes exchange link-state information only with their $k$-hop neighbors. After link outcomes are realized in each slot, Q-GUARD builds per-link purification cost tables from realized Bell pairs, allocates per-hop fidelity targets using a Werner-state equal-split rule, and selects between candidate path segments using a segment-local expected-goodput (EXG) metric that jointly accounts for swap success, purification overhead, and resource availability. We also introduce Q-GUARD-WS, an extension that exploits per-link hardware quality estimates to allocate purification effort non-uniformly across hops. On synthetic 100-node topologies with heterogeneous link fidelity and stochastic BBPSSW purification, Q-GUARD raises the qualified success rate from under 20\% to over 85\% on 4-hop paths and nearly doubles the qualified service radius in Euclidean distance relative to throughput-only and naive-purification baselines, while Q-GUARD-WS provides additional throughput gains under high hardware heterogeneity.

</details>


### [27] [Rethinking Network Topologies for Cost-Effective Mixture-of-Experts LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00254)
*Junsun Choi,Sam Son,Sunjin Choi,Hansung Kim,Yakun Sophia Shao,Scott Shenker,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Borivoje Nikolic*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究发现MoE大模型服务中，低成本的五交换机拓扑（如3D full-mesh）比传统的scale-up拓扑更具成本效益，提升20.6-56.2%；当前链路带宽过度配置，降低带宽可提高性价比达27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构使LLM服务成为集群级工作负载，通信消耗大量运行时间，促使行业投入昂贵的高带宽扩展网络。作者质疑这种昂贵基础设施是否绝对必要，旨在系统分析网络成本效益。

Method: 首次对MoE LLM服务的网络成本效益进行系统跨层分析，比较四种代表性XPU拓扑（scale-up、scale-out、3D torus和3D full-mesh），分析不同服务场景下的性能-成本权衡。

Result: 低成本无交换机拓扑在所有服务场景中比scale-up拓扑更具成本效益，提升20.6-56.2%；3D full-mesh拓扑在性能-成本权衡中达到帕累托最优；当前scale-up链路带宽过度配置，降低带宽可使性价比提升达27%；未来GPU代际分析表明无交换机网络的成本-性能优势将持续。

Conclusion: 昂贵的scale-up网络基础设施对MoE LLM服务并非绝对必要，低成本的开关较少拓扑结构更具成本效益，为工业界LLM服务集群网络架构设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures have turned LLM serving into a cluster-scale workload in which communication consumes a considerable portion of LLM serving runtime. This has prompted industry to invest heavily in expensive high-bandwidth scale-up networks. We question whether such costly infrastructure is strictly necessary. We present the first systematic cross-layer analysis of network cost-effectiveness for MoE LLM serving, comparing four representative XPU (e.g., GPU/TPU) topologies (scale-up, scale-out, 3D torus, and 3D full-mesh). We find that lower-cost switchless topologies are more cost-effective than the scale-up topology across all serving scenarios explored, improving cost-effectiveness by 20.6-56.2%. In particular, the 3D full-mesh topology is Pareto-optimal in terms of the performance-cost tradeoff. We also find that current scale-up link bandwidths are over-provisioned: reducing the link bandwidth improves throughput per cost by up to 27%. A forward-looking analysis of upcoming GPU generations indicates that the cost-performance advantage of switchless networks will likely persist.

</details>


### [28] [A Policy-Driven DRL Framework for System-Level Tradeoff Control in NR-U/Wi-Fi Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00457)
*Po-Heng Chou,Yi-Fang Yu,Shou-Yu Chen,Chiapin Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的策略驱动型TXOP控制框架，通过马尔可夫决策过程建模和深度Q网络学习，实现了NR-U与Wi-Fi异构共存网络中公平性、吞吐量和服务质量之间的灵活权衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NR-U与Wi-Fi在非授权频谱共存时，由于异构信道接入机制导致频谱利用率严重失衡，Wi-Fi性能显著下降，亟需一种系统级的资源协调方案来解决这一问题。

Method: 将共存过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，采用深度Q网络(DQN)通过在线交互学习控制策略；核心创新在于通过奖励设计引入策略层，实现了对公平性、吞吐量和服务质量之间系统级权衡的显式控制，并开发了绝对公平、适度公平和基于效用三种策略以达成不同运行点。

Result: 仿真结果表明：在严格公平控制下，Jain公平指数超过0.9；与绝对公平策略相比，适度公平策略将聚合吞吐量提升68.22%；基于效用的策略进一步将效用值提升177.6%。

Conclusion: 策略驱动控制为异构共存网络中的权衡管理提供了一种灵活且有效的解决方案，能够根据不同需求实现对网络性能的精细化调控。

Abstract: The coexistence of NR-U and Wi-Fi in unlicensed spectrum introduces a system-level resource coordination problem, where heterogeneous channel access mechanisms lead to a significant imbalance in spectrum utilization and degraded Wi-Fi performance. To address this challenge, we propose a policy-driven deep reinforcement learning (DRL) framework for adaptive TXOP control, in which the coexistence process is formulated as a Markov decision process (MDP) and a deep Q-network (DQN) learns control policies through online interaction. A key contribution is the introduction of a policy layer via reward design, enabling explicit control of system-level tradeoffs among fairness, throughput, and quality of service (QoS). Three policies, namely absolute fairness, moderate fairness, and utility-based fairness, are developed to achieve different operating points. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves a Jain fairness index above 0.9 under strict fairness control. Compared to absolute fairness, moderate fairness improves aggregate throughput by 68.22%, while the utility-based policy further enhances utility by 177.6%. These results demonstrate that policy-driven control provides a flexible and effective solution for managing tradeoffs in heterogeneous coexistence networks.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Per-Request QoS: Coordinating Industrial Workflows with B5G/6G Network Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00570)
*Qize Guo,Bjoern Riemer,Tarik Taleb,Yan Chen,Hao Yu,Hemant Zope*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业服务工作流的能力感知协调框架，通过网络提前暴露可支持的服务质量（QoS）配置文件，工业侧据此规划需求轨迹，变被动请求为主动协调，从而显著提升了服务连续性与任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的B5G/6G工业服务交互主要基于请求驱动模式，即在工作流每个阶段单独请求QoS，缺乏对未来需求和网络近期能力的考量。这种供需不匹配限制了双方的规划能力，容易导致可预见的不兼容甚至服务中断。

Method: 提出一种能力感知协调框架。在一个有界的规划窗口内，网络侧公开其可持续支持的QoS配置文件，工业侧将即将到来的工作流阶段映射到这些已披露的能力上，并提交需求轨迹进行联合评估；同时支持在网络状况变化时进行协调更新。

Result: 通过在真实B5G系统上的工业视频检测案例研究及大规模仿真验证，该协调机制能够提高服务连续性，减少破坏性的服务拒绝，并显著提升重负载下的工作流完成率。

Conclusion: 未来的工业网络应超越被动的、基于单一请求的QoS处理模式，转向前瞻性的、能力感知的、工作流级别的协调机制。

Abstract: Beyond-5G (B5G) and 6G networks are expected to enable more complex industrial services, which often operate according to multi-phase workflows with phase-specific communication requirements. However, current interaction between applications and networks remains predominantly request-driven: Quality of Service (QoS) is requested at each workflow phase transition and evaluated independently, without explicit consideration of upcoming demand or network's near-term capability. This mismatch limits the ability of both sides to plan ahead, often resulting in foreseeable incompatibilities, even service disruptions. This article presents a capability-aware coordination framework for workflow-based industrial services. Within a bounded planning window, the network exposes the QoS profiles it can sustainably support, while the industrial side maps upcoming workflow phases to these disclosed capabilities and submits the resulting demand trajectory for joint assessment. The framework also supports coordinated updates when network conditions change during execution. An industrial video inspection case study on a real B5G system, complemented by large-scale simulation, illustrates that such coordination can improve service continuity, reduce disruptive rejections, and increase workflow completion under heavy load. The results suggest that future industrial networking should move beyond reactive per-request QoS handling toward forward-looking, capability-aware, workflow-level coordination.

</details>


### [30] [Inductive Latent Context Persistence: Closing the Post-Handover Cold Start in 6G Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00593)
*Anubhab Banerjee,Daniyal Amir Awan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出ILCP框架解决GNN切换模型在切换后的冷启动问题，通过压缩传输循环状态实现零乒乓切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的切换决策仅依赖UE测量导致结果不一致；现有GNN方法在切换时丢弃循环状态导致目标基站需从零重建模型，面临冷启动问题。

Method: 提出归纳式潜在上下文持久化(ILCP)：将源循环状态压缩为128字节负载通过3GPP Xn接口传输，在目标gNB自适应；将RAN建模为UE节点、gNB节点、测量边和Xn边构成的动态异构图。

Result: 在维也纳4G/5G路测中，ILCP实现0.0%乒乓切换（无迁移基线6.5%、Transformer基线22.6%）；切换后50-250ms窗口准确率平均提升5.1pp（峰值+13.3pp）；在GTX 1080上端到端延迟7.7ms(p99)；在扰动下HOF保持10-13%范围，而A3/A5规则HOF达57-65%。

Conclusion: ILCP成功解决了切换后冷启动问题，显著优于传统规则和现有方法，证明了状态迁移的有效性。

Abstract: In modern radio access networks (RANs), rule-based handover (HO) decisions (e.g., A3/A5) depend on user equipment (UE) measurements only, so UEs at the same location can receive inconsistent HO outcomes. GNN-based methods improve HO KPIs using richer context than measurements alone. However, recurrent or graph models discard the per-UE recurrent state at HO and reinitialize at the target next-generation Node B (gNB), losing mobility history and forcing the target model to rebuild from post-HO measurements only. We address this post-HO cold start with Inductive Latent Context Persistence (ILCP), compressing the source recurrent state, transporting it on the 3GPP Xn as a 128-byte payload, and adapting it at the target gNB. We model the RAN as a dynamic heterogeneous graph over UE nodes, gNB nodes, measurement edges, and Xn edges. On a Vienna 4G/5G drive-test, ILCP achieves 0.0% ping-pong HOs versus 6.5% for an identical no-transfer baseline and 22.6% for a Transformer baseline; post-HO accuracy improves by +5.1 pp on average (peak +13.3 pp) in the 50-250 ms window. On one NVIDIA GTX 1080 (8 GB), ILCP runs end-to-end at 7.7 ms p99 per handover decision. Under perturbations (shadow fading, NLOS blockage, SSB-burst sparsity), robustly trained ILCP keeps handover failure (HOF) in the 10-13% range. Under the same fixed-reference-label setting, A3/A5 rises from 1.1% to 57-65% HOF when measurements are perturbed, exposing limits of measurement-only rules.

</details>


### [31] [EASE: Federated Multimodal Unlearning via Entanglement-Aware Anchor Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00733)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出EASE框架，通过双边位移、余弦-正弦分解和方向选择性遗忘锁来解决联邦多模态学习中的解耦遗忘难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态学习中，联合嵌入训练导致遗忘知识在模态间和客户端梯度子空间中纠缠，阻碍了联邦遗忘。现有方法既未切断双线性耦合介导的跨模态重建通道，也未将遗忘专用更新方向与保留客户端共享方向分离。

Method: 基于锚点原则，提出EASE框架：1) 模态层面采用双边位移关闭跨模态重建通道；2) 通过客户端更新子空间的余弦-正弦分解解决子空间纠缠，隔离遗忘专用方向；3) 提出方向选择性遗忘锁约束跨轮次的残余漂移。

Result: 在多个数据集和遗忘场景下表现优异。例如在Flickr30K数据集上使用CLIP-B/32进行客户端遗忘时，遗忘侧和保留侧的R@1分别与重训练参考值相差在0.2和4.2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: EASE框架成功解决了联邦多模态遗忘中由双线性跨模态耦合、主角度子空间纠缠和持续联邦更新产生的三个残余锚点通道问题，实现了有效的知识遗忘。

Abstract: Federated Multimodal Learning (FML) trains multimodal models across decentralized clients while keeping their image-text pairs private. However, joint embedding training entangles forgotten knowledge across both modalities and client gradient subspaces, hindering federated unlearning. Previous federated unlearning approaches neither sever the cross-modal reconstruction channel mediated by bilinear coupling nor separate forget-exclusive update directions from those shared with retained clients. We identify an Anchor Principle for federated multimodal contrastive unlearning: forgotten alignments persist through three residual anchors arising from bilinear cross-modal coupling, principal-angle subspace entanglement, and continued federated updates. At the modality level, we show that bilateral displacement of both visual and language branches closes the cross-modal reconstruction channel. Correspondingly, our method addresses subspace entanglement through Cosine--Sine decomposition of client-update subspaces, isolating forget-exclusive directions from retain support. Moreover, we propose a direction-selective Forget Lock that bounds residual drift across rounds. Combining these strategies, we present EASE, an Entanglement-Aware Subspace Excision framework that closes all three anchor channels under a unified design. EASE demonstrates consistent superiority across multiple datasets and unlearning scenarios, for instance, matching the retrain reference to within 0.2 and 4.2 R@1 points on the forget and retain sides under client unlearning on Flickr30K with CLIP-B/32.

</details>


### [32] [AdvNet: Revealing Performance Issues in Network Protocols by Generating Adversarial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00755)
*Shehab Sarar Ahmed,William Sentosa,Yinjie Zhang,Yoav Lebendiker,Michael Shnaiderman,Tomer Gilad,Nathan H. Jay,Brighten Godfrey,Michael Schapira*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AdvNet系统，利用机器学习自动生成对抗性网络环境，成功发现了27个拥塞控制协议实现中的Linux内核缺陷和隐藏局限性，证明了自动化对抗测试在协议开发中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前协议设计者依赖手工设计的测试用例或真实环境捕获的数据集来评估拥塞控制协议性能，这种方法可能无意中忽略了算法在意外环境或工作负载下的关键行为特征，导致潜在问题未被发现。

Method: 提出AdvNet系统，采用基于机器学习的优化技术自动生成对抗性网络环境，使目标协议实现表现不佳；系统集成了鲁棒的噪声处理机制，以缓解真实世界协议性能中固有的变异性。

Result: 成功为27个内核空间的单路径和多路径拥塞控制协议实现创建了对抗性场景；发现了导致Linux内核缺陷的问题网络条件；揭示了CC实现中隐藏的局限性；提供了关于协议鲁棒性的洞察。

Conclusion: 自动化对抗测试可以成为协议开发中的宝贵工具，鲁棒性是基准测试拥塞控制协议的一个有用新维度，该方法有助于在协议部署前发现潜在问题。

Abstract: Infrastructure protocols like Congestion Control (CC) seek to provide reliable performance across a wide range of Internet environments. Currently, protocol designers assess performance through hand-designed test cases or data sets captured from real environments. However, such approaches may inadvertently overlook critical facets of the algorithm's behavior when they encounter an unanticipated environment or workload.
  We seek to understand the unanticipated with \sys, a system that automatically generates adversarial network environments that cause a target protocol implementation to perform poorly. AdvNet employs machine learning-based optimization to generate environments, and incorporates a robust noise-handling mechanism to mitigate the variability inherent in real-world protocol performance. Although our approach is more general, this paper focuses specifically on transport protocols and their CC implementations. We showcase AdvNet's capability to create adversarial scenarios for 27 kernel-space implementations of both single-path and multi-path CC protocols, for several use cases with different performance goals. AdvNet identifies problematic network conditions that expose previously unnoticed Linux kernel bugs and uncovers hidden limitations in CC implementations, and provides insights about robustness. These results suggest that automated adversarial testing can be a valuable tool in protocol development, and that robustness is a useful new dimension for benchmarking CC protocols.

</details>
