### [33] [Shooting Neutrons at Neurons: Radiation Testing of a Spiking Neural Network on Flash-Based FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00030)
*Wim Nijsink,Bruno Endres Forlin,Amirreza Yousefzadeh,Marco Ottavi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种针对神经形态处理器的辐射测试方法，发现启用了SDSP在线学习的处理器在高能中子辐射下能显著延长失效时间并具备部分恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态（脉冲）处理器正逐渐被应用于太空和航空等恶劣、辐射易发的环境中，这些场景对能效和优雅降级有严格要求。然而，目前缺乏专门针对具备片上突触可塑性神经形态处理器的辐射测试方法与可靠性评估。

Method: 研究者将开源的ODIN脉冲神经网络处理器（配备脉冲依赖突触可塑性SDSP）映射到FPGA上，将其暴露于高能中子束中进行辐射实验。实验过程中持续监测MNIST分类精度并记录突触状态，以此提取突触存储器的SEU（单粒子翻转）截面，并开发校准故障模型以指导故障注入活动。研究者对比了仅推理和在线学习两种配置下的表现。

Result: 实验提取了ODIN突触存储器的SEU截面。通过对比发现，启用SDSP在线学习机制可以显著延长应用级失效时间，并能从累积的位翻转中实现部分恢复，且硬件开销较小。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了所提出的辐射测试方法的有效性，并揭示了在线学习机制（SDSP）对提升神经形态处理器在辐射环境下可靠性的积极作用，为恶劣环境下的神经形态计算应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Neuromorphic, or spiking, processors are increasingly being considered for use in harsh, radiation-prone environments such as space and avionics, where energy efficiency and graceful degradation are essential. In this study, we propose and experimentally validate a radiation-testing methodology specifically designed for neuromorphic processors that employ on-chip synaptic plasticity. We map the open-source ODIN SNN processor with Spike-Dependent Synaptic Plasticity (SDSP) onto the FPGA and expose it to a high-energy neutron beam while continuously monitoring MNIST classification accuracy and recording the synaptic state. From these measurements, we extract SEU cross-sections for ODIN's synaptic memory and develop a calibrated fault model to inform a complementary fault-injection campaign. By comparing inference-only and online-learning configurations, we demonstrate that enabling SDSP can significantly extend the time to application-level failure and enable partial recovery from accumulated bit flips, with modest hardware overhead.

</details>


### [34] [ROSA: Robust and Energy-Efficient Microring-Based Optical Neural Networks via Optical Shift-and-Add and Layer-Wise Hybrid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00032)
*Huifan Zhang,Yun Hu,Caizhi Sheng,Yurui Qu,Pingqiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSA架构，通过光移位累加模块和层级混合映射策略，在微环光神经网络中显著提升了鲁棒性与能效，并大幅改善了图像识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微环谐振器（MRR）光神经网络在实现高性能计算时，面临器件噪声（如DAC和热变化）、阵列规模设计及数据流映射等挑战，亟需提升系统的鲁棒性和能效。

Method: 提出ROSA架构，引入光移位累加（OSA）模块与层级混合映射策略；构建噪声感知的电压-权重模型以应对DAC与热变化；提出负载感知框架，协同优化MRR阵列规模与层级数据流。

Result: 优化后的阵列相比DEAP-CNNs和通用紧凑阵列，聚合相对能量延迟积（EDP）分别降低64%和26%；OSA模块额外贡献29%的EDP降低；混合映射策略使CIFAR-10精度较权重静止映射提升8.3%，平均EDP比DEAP-CNNs降低54.7%。

Conclusion: ROSA架构有效解决了微环光神经网络中的噪声敏感性与能效瓶颈问题，在保持高识别精度的同时显著降低了能耗延迟，为高性能光子神经网络设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work presents ROSA, a microring-based optical neural network architecture that improves robustness and energy efficiency using an optical shift-and-add (OSA) module and a layer-wise hybrid mapping strategy. It introduces a noise-aware voltage-to-weight model considering DAC and thermal variations, and a workload-aware framework to co-optimize MRR array size and layer-wise dataflow. Optimized arrays reduce the aggregated relative energy-delay product (EDP) by 64% and 26% compared with DEAP-CNNs and a general compact array, respectively. OSA further contributes 29% EDP reduction. The proposed hybrid mapping strategy improves CIFAR-10 accuracy by 8.3% over weight-stationary mapping while achieving an average 54.7% lower EDP than DEAP-CNNs.

</details>


### [35] [Lottery BP: Unlocking Quantum Error Decoding at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00038)
*Yanzhang Zhu,Chen-Yu Peng,Yun Hao Chen,Yeong-Luh Ueng,Di Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lottery BP解码器、PolyQec架构和Syndrilla模拟器，在准确性、速度和可扩展性方面显著优于现有方法，为大规模量子计算的容错解码提供了综合解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在百万量子比特规模上实现实时容错，需要可扩展的解码方案。现有解码算法（如聚类、匹配、置信传播BP、神经网络）在广泛的量子纠错码（表面码、环面码、双变量自行车码）上存在准确性不足、成本高昂或兼容性差等问题，无法同时满足准确、快速、通用和可扩展的理想解码器要求。

Method: 1) 提出Lottery BP解码器，在解码过程中引入随机性，并采用征候投票作为预处理步骤压缩多轮测量；2) 设计PolyQec架构，将Lottery BP作为局部解码器、有序统计解码(OSD)作为全局解码器，可配置于表面码/环面码和X/Z校验；3) 开发基于PyTorch的模块化解码模拟器Syndrilla，支持灵活扩展新解码器并集成多种性能指标。

Result: Lottery BP在拓扑码上的解码准确性相比BP提高2~8个数量级；PolyQec架构调用昂贵的全局OSD解码器频率比BP+OSD降低3~5个数量级；运行于GPU的Syndrilla模拟器比CPU快1~2个数量级；征候投票增加了解码延迟裕度并缓解了积压问题。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了现有解码器与理想解码器之间的差距，通过Lottery BP解码器、PolyQec架构和Syndrilla模拟器三个方面的创新，在保持通用性的同时显著提升了量子纠错解码的准确性、速度和可扩展性，为大规模量子计算系统的实时容错提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: To enable fault tolerance on millions of qubits in real time, scalable decoding is necessary, which motivates this paper. Existing decoding algorithms (decoders), such as clustering, matching, belief propagation (BP), and neural networks, suffer from one or more of inaccuracy, costliness, and incompatibility, upon a broad set of quantum error correction codes, such as surface code, toric code, and bivariate bicycle code. Therefore, there exists a gap between existing decoders and an ideal decoder that is accurate, fast, general, and scalable simultaneously. This paper contributes in three aspects, including decoder, decoder architecture, and decoding simulator. First, we propose Lottery BP, a decoder that introduces randomness during decoding. Lottery BP improves the decoding accuracy over BP by 2~8 orders of magnitude for topological codes. To efficiently decode multi-round measurement errors, we propose syndrome vote as a pre-processing step before Lottery BP, which compresses multiple rounds of syndromes into one. Syndrome voting increases the latency margin of decoding and mitigates the backlog problem. Second, we design a PolyQec architecture that implements Lottery BP as a local decoder and ordered statistics decoding (OSD) as a global decoder, and it is configurable for surface/toric code and X/Z check. Since Lottery BP boosts the local decoding accuracy, PolyQec invokes the costly global OSD decoder less frequently over BP+OSD to enhance the scalability, e.g., 3~5 orders of magnitude less for topological codes. Third, to evaluate decoders fairly, we develop a PyTorch-based decoding simulator, Syndrilla, that modularizes the simulation pipeline and allows to extend new decoders flexibly. We formulate multiple metrics to quantify the performance of decoders and integrate them in Syndrilla. Running on GPUs, Syndrilla is 1~2 orders of magnitude faster than CPUs.

</details>


### [36] [Autoformalizing Memory Specifications with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00058)
*Jan Ole Ernst,Dmitri Michelangelo Saberi,Derek Christ,Thomas Zimmermann,Rajath Salegame,Suhaas M. Bhat,Stanislav Levental,Thomas Dybdahl Ahle,Matthias Jung*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动将自然语言DRAM规范形式化为DRAMPyML的方法，并发布了评估数据集DRAMBench，旨在解决现有AI方法无法进行端到端规范符合性验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计验证（DV）的目标是确保芯片设计实现与规范完全匹配且无功能错误，但现有方法通常依赖大量人工解释将规范文档转换为可测试的形式化表示，且现有AI方法仅关注狭窄、孤立的子任务，无法捕捉真实世界验证的复杂性，缺乏端到端的规范符合性验证能力。

Method: 提出了一种自动形式化方法，能够将行业相关的动态随机存取存储器（DRAM）标准的自然语言规范转换为形式化表示DRAMPyML，该表示可用于下游设计验证任务，包括SystemVerilog断言生成、激励生成和功能覆盖率分析。

Result: 成功实现了自然语言内存芯片规范的自动形式化，生成的DRAMPyML可用于实际的DV任务；同时发布了基准数据集DRAMBench，用于评估硬件自动形式化模型能力的演进和新方法。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了现有AI在设计验证领域缺乏端到端解决方案的空白，通过自动化形式化过程降低了人工成本，提供的基准数据集为未来硬件自动形式化研究提供了评估标准。

Abstract: The primary goal of Design Verification (DV) is to ensure that a proposed chip design implementation (either in code, or physical form) exactly matches its specification and is free of functional errors in order to avoid costly re-designs. Achieving this often demands extensive manual interpretation, translating the specification document into a formal, testable representation. While AI has made progress in DV, current approaches typically focus on narrow, isolated tasks rather than full end-to-end specification compliance of modern chip designs, failing to capture the complexity of real-world verification. Our method automatically formalizes natural language memory chip specifications, for industry relevant Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) standards, into a formal representation called DRAMPyML that can be used for downstream DV tasks like the generation of SystemVerilog assertions, stimulus, and functional coverage. We also release our benchmarking dataset, DRAMBench, which can be used to evaluate the evolution of model capabilities (and new approaches) at hardware autoformalization.

</details>


### [37] [DPU or GPU for Accelerating Neural Networks Inference -- Why not both? Split CNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00174)
*Ali Emre Oztas,Mahir Demir,James Garside,Mikel Luj'an*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Split CNN Inference方法，通过将CNN推理在DPU和GPU之间进行分割，实现了最高3.37倍的延迟降低，并利用GNN模型自动化预测最优分割点，准确率达96.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的视频和图像流处理需要低延迟，现有工作主要关注使用单一硬件单元（如GPU、FPGA、DPU）来加速神经网络，但结合多种硬件单元可以进一步降低延迟。

Method: 提出Split CNN Inference方法，将CNN推理在DPU和GPU之间进行分割：第一部分在Versal VCK190的AI引擎（DPU）上运行，处理初始CNN层；第二部分通过异步流水线在NVIDIA RTX 2080 GPU上运行剩余层，减少了数据源与GPU之间的数据传输。同时提出基于图神经网络（GNN）的分区索引预测方法来自动化CNN分割。

Result: 在LeNet-5、ResNet18/50/101/152、VGG16和MobileNetv2等模型上进行实验，结果表明相比仅使用DPU的执行延迟降低了2.48倍，相比仅使用GPU的执行延迟降低了3.37倍。训练的GNN模型在设备间分割层的准确率达到96.27%。

Conclusion: 通过结合DPU和GPU进行CNN推理分割，可以显著降低边缘设备上的推理延迟，同时GNN模型能够高效自动化地确定最优分割策略，为多硬件协同加速提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Video and image streaming on edge devices requires low latency. To address this, Neural Networks (NNs) are widely used, and prior work mainly focuses on accelerating them with single hardware units such as Graphics Processing Units (GPUs), Field Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs), and Deep Learning Processing Units (DPUs). However, further reductions in latency can be observed by combining these units. In this paper, partitioning CNN inference across DPU and GPU (Split CNN Inference) is proposed. The first partition runs on the AI engines (DPU) of a Versal VCK190, which consists of initial CNN layers processing the input images. The DPU processes the first partition near the source of the data. Pipelined asynchronously, a GPU runs the remaining layers. The GPU (NVIDIA RTX 2080) processes the second partition, albeit having reduced the data transfer between the data source (storage/camera) and the GPU. Furthermore, a Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based partition index prediction method is proposed to automate the partitioning of CNNs needed for the Split Inference. Well established models such as LeNet-5, ResNet18/50/101/152, VGG16, and MobileNetv2 are analyzed. Results demonstrate up to 2.48x latency improvement over DPU-only execution and up to 3.37x over GPU-only execution. The trained GNN model splits the layers between the appropriate devices with 96.27% accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [A PVT-Resilient Subthreshold SRAM-Based In-Memory Computing Accelerator with In-Situ Regulation for Energy-Efficient Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00319)
*Shih-Hang Kao,Yang-Chan Hung,I-Wen Wang,Bing-Han Liu,Yu-Chia Chen,Tian-Sheuan Chang,Shyh-Jye Jou,Chien-Nan Liu,Hung-Ming Chen,Wei-Zen Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向脉冲神经网络(SNN)的PVT鲁棒亚阈值SRAM存内计算(CIM)宏单元，通过集成原位电流传感器和分布式电压调节器实现了大规模亚阈值电流模式CIM，在28nm工艺下实现了1181.42 TOPS/W能效和93.64%关键词识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对边缘设备上脉冲神经网络的高能效处理需求，现有存内计算架构在亚阈值工作区域面临工艺-电压-温度(PVT)敏感性和能效开销问题。由于SNN具有高度稀疏性，亟需开发能够容忍PVT变化且能效优异的专用硬件加速器。

Method: 设计集成原位电流传感器和分布式电压调节器的亚阈值SRAM CIM宏单元(1024字线×1304位线×128神经元)，采用可编程存储单元基发放阈值增强神经元PVT鲁棒性，通过stride-tick批处理调度减少缓冲开销并提升输入数据复用率，充分利用SNN稀疏特性优化系统架构。

Result: 在28nm CMOS工艺下流片验证，原型实现93.64%关键词识别准确率，峰值能效达1181.42 TOPS/W，面积效率为7.24 TOPS/mm²，有效实现了大规模亚阈值电流模式存内计算的PVT鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该亚阈值SRAM CIM宏单元成功解决了PVT敏感性难题，为高性能边缘SNN处理提供了可行且高效的硬件解决方案，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了能效和面积效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a PVT-resilient, subthreshold SRAM-based computing-in-memory (CIM) macro tailored for energy-efficient spiking neural networks (SNNs). The macro integrates in-situ current sensors and distributed voltage regulators to enable robust large-scale (1024 wordlines, 1304 bitlines and 128 shared neuron cells) subthreshold current-mode CIM, mitigating energy overheads and process-voltage-temperature (PVT) sensitivity. The neuron cells adopt a programmable, memory cell-based firing threshold to enhance neuron robustness against PVT variations. The architecture uses a stride-tick batching schedule to significantly reduce buffer overhead with enhanced input data reuse. Exploiting the high sparsity of SNNs, the proposed system demonstrates significant improvements in energy efficiency and variation tolerance. Fabricated in 28-nm CMOS, the prototype attains 93.64\% accuracy on keyword spotting, delivers up to 1181.42 TOPS/W, and achieves 7.24 TOPS/mm^2, demonstrating a viable and efficient solution for high-performance edge SNN processing.

</details>


### [39] [VitaLLM: A Versatile and Tiny Accelerator for Mixed-Precision LLM Inference on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00320)
*Zi-Wei Lin,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VitaLLM，一种面向边缘设备的混合精度加速器，通过结合无乘法器的TINT核心与复用Booth数据通路的BoothFlex核心，并引入预测性稀疏注意力机制，实现了在0.214 mm²面积内高效运行三元权重大语言模型（如BitNet b1.58 3B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署大型语言模型（LLM）面临严峻的存储、计算和能耗限制。虽然三元权重模型（如BitNet b1.58）通过极度量化显著压缩了模型尺寸，但现有硬件无法高效支持混合精度计算（低精度投影与INT8注意力）且受限于KV缓存的访存瓶颈。因此，亟需一种专用加速架构以释放三元权重LLM在边缘端的潜力。

Method: 设计了双核心混合精度架构：TINT核心（无乘法器，专门处理三元-INT投影）与BoothFlex核心（复用基数为4的Booth数据通路，统一支持INT8×INT8注意力和三元-INT计算）。引入预测性稀疏注意力机制，采用前导一（LO）代理和无比较的Top-K选择器，按1-K/M比例剪枝KV读取量。系统集成层面，采用头级流水线与基于absmax的量化屏障，标准化跨核心接口并重叠非线性归约与线性分块计算。

Result: 在16 nm工艺、1 GHz/0.8 V条件下流片的硅原型实现了72.46 tokens/s的解码速度和0.88秒的预填充延迟（64 tokens），核心面积仅0.214 mm²，片上存储120 KB，并在消融实验中有效降低了KV流量并提升了硬件利用率。

Conclusion: VitaLLM成功验证了在边缘级平台上高效推理三元权重大语言模型（BitNet b1.58 3B）的可行性，为未来混合精度LLM加速器设计提供了紧凑且实用的蓝图。

Abstract: We present VitaLLM, a mixed precision accelerator that enables ternary weight large language models to run efficiently on edge devices. The design combines two compute cores, a multiplier free TINT core for ternary-INT projections and a BoothFlex core that reuses a radix-4 Booth datapath for both INT8$\times$INT8 attention and ternary-INT-sustaining utilization without duplicating arrays. A predictive sparse attention mechanism employs a leading-one (LO) surrogate with a comparison-free top-$K$ selector to prune key/value (KV) fetches by roughly $1-K/M$ for $M$ cached tokens, confining exact attention to $K$ candidates. System-level integration uses head-level pipelining and an absmax-based quantization barrier to standardize cross-core interfaces and overlap nonlinear reductions with linear tiles. A 16 nm silicon prototype at 1 GHz/0.8 V achieves 72.46 tokens/s in decode and 0.88 s prefill (64 tokens) within 0.214 mm^2 and 120 KB on-chip memory, while reducing KV traffic and improving utilization in ablations. These results demonstrate practical BitNet b1.58 (3B) inference on edge-class platforms and provide a compact blueprint for future mixed-precision LLM accelerators.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个从FlashAttention-3内核插桩到周期精确仿真的流水线，并修正了现有分析模型在DRAM流量估算上的偏差，仿真器在H800上的平均绝对百分比误差为5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPGPU架构为支持大语言模型引入了warp specialization等新特性，但现有周期精确仿真器未能及时支持如Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA)等新硬件特性，且现有分析模型在某些配置下会错误估计DRAM流量，缺乏满足AI基础设施和计算机架构研究需求的有效工具。

Conclusion: 该仿真流水线和分析方法为现代GPGPU架构上的大语言模型研究提供了可靠的周期精确仿真工具和准确的工作负载特征分析模型。

Abstract: To efficiently support Large Language Models (LLMs), modern GPGPU architectures have introduced new features and programming paradigms, such as warp specialization. These features enable temporal overlap between the producer and consumer, as well as between matrix multiplication and activation function operations, substantially improving performance. To conduct effective AI infrastructure and computer architecture research, cycle-accurate simulators that support these new features, together with analytical models that faithfully capture workload characteristics, are essential.
  However, existing academic tools provide limited support for these emerging requirements. Existing cycle-accurate simulators do not incorporate new NVIDIA GPU features, such as the Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA), in a timely manner. Moreover, existing analytical models can misestimate DRAM traffic under certain configurations.
  In this paper, we build a simulation pipeline from FlashAttention-3 kernel instrumentation to cycle-accurate simulation. The simulator achieves a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.7\% and a maximum absolute percentage error of 12.7\% against H800. We also provide a theoretical analysis of FlashAttention-3 and explain why existing analytical models can produce inaccurate traffic estimates.

</details>

### [41] [Replication in Graph Partitioning and Scheduling Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00209)
*Pál András Papp,Toni Böhnlein,A. N. Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了复制操作对图划分和DAG调度问题的影响。理论方面，复制使图划分的近似复杂度显著增加，而对调度问题影响较小。实验方面，复制操作在图划分中平均降低成本17%-65%，在DAG调度中平均降低11.61%-23.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行计算中，负载均衡与通信开销之间存在权衡。现有研究多假设节点仅分配给单一处理器，但现实中将操作复制到多个处理器可小幅增加计算成本，却能显著降低通信需求。因此需要全面分析复制对划分和调度问题的影响。

Method: 理论分析：研究复制对图划分近似复杂度和调度问题复杂度的影响。实验分析：对于超图划分，使用整数线性规划（ILP）比较最优成本；对于DAG调度，在小规模图上使用ILP，并开发了适用于大规模工作负载的复杂启发式算法。

Result: 理论结果：复制使图划分问题的近似复杂度显著增加，但对调度问题复杂度影响较小。实验结果：超图划分中，复制平均降低成本17%-65%，某些情况下可完全消除通信需求；DAG调度中，复制平均降低成本11.61%-23.13%，最高可达58.17%。

Conclusion: 复制操作在并行计算的图划分和DAG调度中具有显著的优化潜力。尽管复制增加了图划分的理论复杂度，但在实际应用中能大幅降低通信成本，为并行计算优化提供了新的思路和实用方法。

Abstract: The efficient parallel execution of complex computations requires balancing the workload across processors while minimizing the communication between them. This inherent trade-off is often captured by graph partitioning or DAG scheduling problems. For the sake of model simplicity, most works on these problems assume that nodes can be assigned to only a single processor. However, in reality, replicating an operation on several processors can easily be beneficial: it may increase the computational costs only by a small amount, while significantly reducing the communication requirements.
  Our goal is to provide a comprehensive analysis of the impact of replication on partitioning and scheduling problems. On the theoretical side, we show that for graph partitioning, replication makes the problem significantly harder in terms of approximation complexity, whereas for scheduling, its impact on complexity seems less prominent. On the experimental side, we conduct a thorough analysis of the cost reduction obtainable by replication, on a wide range of graphs from real-world applications. For hypergraph partitioning, we use Integer Linear Programming (ILP) formulations to compare the optimal costs; our experiments show that replication can reduce the cost by 17%-65% on average, or even entirely remove the need for communication in some cases. For DAG scheduling, we similarly use ILPs on smaller graphs, and develop a sophisticated heuristic that is also applicable to much larger workloads. Our experiments here demonstrate a mean cost reduction of 11.61%-23.13% with replication, or even up to 58.17% in some cases.

</details>

*P. Rosciszewski,A. Krzywaniak,S. Iserte,K. Rojek,P. Gepner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了IPU在AI辅助计算流体动力学仿真中的应用，通过优化数据流水线实现34%加速，并展示了从2卡到16卡的良好扩展性（5倍吞吐量提升）。

Method: 使用Poplar SDK提供的定制版TensorFlow，将训练程序适配到IPU-POD16平台。通过popdist库优化主机端向IPU传输训练数据的流水线，解决数据供给瓶颈。采用数据并行策略测试不同IPU数量下的性能表现。

Result: 1）基于OpenFOAM仿真数据训练的模型能够准确预测仿真状态；2）利用popdist库优化数据供给流程，实现高达34%的加速；3）从1卡扩展到2卡时，通信开销导致吞吐量无提升；4）从2卡扩展到16卡时，吞吐量从560.8提升至2805.8 samples/s，展现了良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: IPU在AI for simulation领域具有应用价值。虽然小规模部署存在通信开销问题，但一旦跨过初始开销，IPU的片间通信能力支持良好的扩展性，适合需要高吞吐量的机器学习辅助仿真训练任务。

Abstract: Intelligence Processing Units (IPU) have proven useful for many AI applications. In this paper, we evaluate them within the emerging field of \emph{AI for simulation}, where traditional numerical simulations are supported by artificial intelligence approaches. We focus specifically on a program for training machine learning models supporting a \emph{computational fluid dynamics} application. We use custom TensorFlow provided by the Poplar SDK to adapt the program for the IPU-POD16 platform and investigate its ease of use and performance scalability. Training a model on data from OpenFOAM simulations allows us to get accurate simulation state predictions in test time. We show how to utilize the \emph{popdist} library to overcome a performance bottleneck in feeding training data to the IPU on the host side, achieving up to 34\% speedup. Due to communication overheads, using data parallelism to utilize two IPUs instead of one does not improve the throughput. However, once the intra-IPU costs have been paid, the hardware capabilities for inter-IPU communication allow for good scalability. Increasing the number of IPUs from 2 to 16 improves the throughput from 560.8 to 2805.8 samples/s.

</details>


### [43] [Space Network of Experts: Architecture and Expert Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00515)
*Zhanwei Wang,Huiling Yang,Min Sheng,Khaled B. Letaief,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Space-XNet框架，通过两层优化放置策略（层放置和专家放置）在卫星网络中分布式部署大规模混合专家模型，相比传统方法实现至少3倍延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太空数据中心因能持续高效收集太阳能，被视为执行大规模语言模型的有前景平台。然而，卫星网络中有限的计算和通信资源使得大规模LLM的分布式部署面临挑战，模型架构与网络拓扑的协调问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出Space-XNet框架，采用两层放置策略：(1)层放置：利用自回归推理的环形通信模式，将卫星星座按轨道方向划分为环形排列的子网，每个子网托管一个MoE层；(2)专家放置：建立优化问题，将异构激活概率的专家映射到卫星，得出"高频激活专家应映射到低期望延迟路由路径上的卫星"原则。

Result: 在千颗卫星星座规模的实验中，Space-XNet相比传统随机放置和消融放置策略实现了至少3倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的Space-XNet框架有效解决了卫星网络中大规模MoE模型的分布式部署问题，通过创新的环形子网架构和优化放置策略显著降低了推理延迟，为太空数据中心执行大规模AI模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Leveraging continuous solar energy harvesting at high efficiency, space data centers are envisioned as a promising platform for executing energy-intensive large language models (LLMs). Recognizing this advantage, space and AI conglomerates (e.g., SpaceX, Google) are actively investing in this vision. One key challenge, however, is the efficient distributed deployment of a large-scale LLM in a satellite network due to the limited onboard computing and communication resources. This gives rise to a placement problem that involves partitioning and mapping model components to satellites such that the fundamentally different model architecture and network topology can be reconciled to ensure low-latency token generation. To address this problem, we present the Space Network of Experts (Space-XNet) framework targeting the distributed execution of a popular mixture-of-experts (MoE) model in space. The proposed placement strategies are two-level: (1) layer placement, which assigns MoE layers to satellite subnets; and (2) intra-layer expert placement, which assigns individual experts to satellites associated with the same layer/subnet. For layer placement, we exploit the ring-like communication pattern of autoregressive inference to partition the satellite constellation along the orbiting direction into subnets arranged on a ring, each hosting one MoE layer. Based on this architecture, we formulate and solve an optimization problem for intra-layer expert placement to map experts with heterogeneous activation probabilities onto satellites. The derived strategy reveals an intuitive principle: a frequently activated expert should be mapped to a satellite on a routing path with low expected latency. Experiments over a thousand-satellite constellation show that Space-XNet achieves at least a threefold latency reduction compared with conventional random and ablation-based placement strategies.

</details>


### [44] [SAGA: Workflow-Atomic Scheduling for AI Agent Inference on GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00528)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出程序级调度范式SAGA，通过预测KV缓存复用、会话亲和性批处理和公平性指标，将复合AI任务完成时间减少1.64倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU调度器将AI代理的链式LLM调用视为独立请求，丢弃了大量可复用的中间状态（KV缓存），导致端到端延迟增加3-8倍。这种请求级抽象与复合AI工作负载的特性根本不匹配。

Method: 提出SAGA分布式调度器，实现程序级调度抽象：1) 构建Agent执行图预测跨工具调用边界的KV缓存复用；2) 采用会话亲和性批处理与工作窃取机制，在保持全局负载均衡的同时共置相关请求；3) 引入Agent公平分享指标，提供可证明的有界偏差保证。

Result: 在64 GPU集群上，相比带前缀缓存和亲和性路由的vLLM，SAGA将任务完成时间减少1.64倍（几何平均），GPU内存利用率提升1.22倍，多租户干扰下SLO达成率达99.2%。代价是峰值吞吐量降低约30%。

Conclusion: 工作流感知调度对于高效的复合AI服务至关重要，尽管在峰值吞吐量上有所折衷，但该权衡适合延迟敏感的交互式部署场景。

Abstract: AI agents execute tens to hundreds of chained LLM calls per task, yet GPU schedulers treat each call as independent, discarding gigabytes of intermediate state between steps and inflating end-to-end latency by 3-8x. We argue that this request-level abstraction is fundamentally mismatched to compound AI workloads, and propose a shift to program-level scheduling: treating the entire agent workflow (not individual inference calls) as the first-class schedulable unit. We present SAGA, a distributed scheduler that implements this abstraction through three mechanisms: (1) Agent Execution Graphs that capture workflow structure to predict KV cache reuse across tool-call boundaries, achieving within 1.31x of Bélády's optimal offline policy; (2) session-affinity batching with work stealing that co-locates correlated requests while maintaining global load balance; and (3) Agent Fair Share, a task-completion-time fairness metric with provable bounded-deviation guarantees. On a 64-GPU cluster serving SWE-bench coding agents and WebArena browser tasks, SAGA reduces task completion time by 1.64x (geometric mean, p < 0.001) over vLLM v0.15.1 with prefix caching and affinity routing, while improving GPU memory utilization by 1.22x and achieving 99.2% SLO attainment under multi-tenant interference. These latency gains come at a quantified cost: approximately 30% lower peak throughput than throughput-optimal batch scheduling, a tradeoff appropriate for the latency-sensitive interactive deployments that dominate compound AI usage. Our results demonstrate that workflow-aware scheduling is essential for efficient compound AI serving.

</details>


### [45] [Tempus: A Temporally Scalable Resource-Invariant GEMM Streaming Framework for Versal AI Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00536)
*M. Grailoo,J. Núñez-Yáñez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Tempus框架，一种面向AMD Versal AI Edge SoC的资源不变时域GEMM加速方案，在保持固定16核计算规模下实现607 GOPS性能，相比空间扩展SOTA方案提升211.2倍效率，同时显著降低资源与功耗开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的质量随计算规模提升，但边缘部署受限于计算、内存和功耗约束。GEMM占推理时间高达90%，现有SOTA框架采用空间扩展策略，在资源受限的边缘SoC上遭遇物理实现失败、带宽饱和和资源消耗过大等问题，亟需高效的边缘GEMM加速方案。

Method: 提出资源不变的时域GEMM框架Tempus：采用固定16个AIE-ML核的计算块，通过迭代图执行和可编程逻辑中的算法数据分块与复制实现可扩展性；利用高速级联流实现II=1的低延迟部分和归约；设计无死锁DATAFLOW协议最大化传输-计算重叠与PLIO复用。

Result: 在AMD Versal AI Edge SoC上实现607 GOPS性能，总片上功耗10.677 W；PAU度量下 prominency factor 较空间SOTA(ARIES)高211.2倍；URAM/DSP利用率为0.00%，核心节省22.0倍、功耗节省7.1倍、I/O需求降低6.3倍。

Conclusion: Tempus通过时域扩展而非空间扩展的策略，成功解决了边缘SoC上GEMM加速的资源约束问题，为边缘LLM推理建立了可持续、可扩展的基础，证明了资源不变设计范式在边缘AI部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs) establish that model quality improves with computational scale, yet edge deployment imposes strict constraints on compute, memory, and power. Since General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) accounts for up to 90\% of inference time, efficient GEMM acceleration is critical for edge AI. The Adaptive Intelligent Engines available in the AMD Versal adaptive SoCs are well suited for this task, but existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) frameworks maximize performance through spatial scaling, distributing workloads across hundreds of cores -- an approach that fails on resource-limited edge SoCs due to physical implementation failures, bandwidth saturation, and excessive resource consumption. We propose Tempus, a Resource-Invariant Temporal GEMM framework for the AMD Versal AI Edge SoC. Rather than expanding hardware resources with matrix size, Tempus employs a fixed compute block of 16 AIE-ML cores, achieving scalability through iterative graph execution and algorithmic data tiling and replication in the Programmable Logic. High-speed cascade streaming ensures low-latency partial sum reduction at Initiation Interval (II) of 1, while a deadlock-free DATAFLOW protocol maximizes transfer-compute overlap and PLIO reuse. Evaluated on GEMM workloads, Tempus achieves 607 GOPS at 10.677 W total on-chip power. By characterizing system-level efficiency through the Platform-Aware Utility (PAU) metric, we prove that Tempus achieves a 211.2x higher prominence factor than the leading spatial SOTA (ARIES). Furthermore, the framework maintains a 0.00\% utilization of URAM/DSP, yielding 22.0x core frugality, 7.1x power frugality, and a 6.3x reduction in I/O demand, establishing a sustainable, scalable foundation for edge LLM inference.

</details>


### [46] [LLM-Emu: Native Runtime Emulation of LLM Inference via Profile-Driven Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00616)
*Wei Da,Evangelia Kalyvianaki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了LLM-Emu，一个面向vLLM的服务原生仿真器，通过仅用采样延迟和合成输出替代GPU前向执行，实现了低成本高精度的在线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统的真实在线评估需要GPU资源且成本高昂；现有仿真器多为离线模式或需重写调度器、依赖精确的算子级延迟模型，无法很好地模拟真实在线服务行为。

Method: 提出LLM-Emu仿真器，保留vLLM的生产级HTTP接口、调度逻辑、KV-cache及输出处理路径，仅将GPU前向计算替换为基于Profile采样的延迟和合成输出Token。

Result: 在两种GPU、四种模型变体、两种模型族、两种注意力后端及Poisson和突发ShareGPT负载下测试，TPOT和ITL绝对误差在4.8%以内，端到端延迟在5.3%以内，输出吞吐量在1.9%以内；TTFT误差最大为10.4%，反映了其对准入和排队状态的敏感性。

Conclusion: 轻量级、服务原生仿真能够有效支持LLM服务系统的在线实验，可作为昂贵GPU实验的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Realistic evaluation of LLM serving systems requires online workloads, dynamic arrivals, queueing, and the serving engine's local scheduling for execution batching, but running such experiments on GPUs is expensive. Existing simulators reduce this cost, but often operate offline or in time-warped mode, re-implement serving-engine schedulers, or require accurate operator/kernel-level latency models. We present LLM-Emu, a serving-native emulator for vLLM that preserves the production HTTP, scheduling, KV-cache, and output-processing paths while replacing only GPU forward execution with profile-sampled latency and synthetic output tokens. Tested on two different GPUs, four model variants, two model families, two attention backends, and both Poisson and bursty ShareGPT workloads, LLM-Emu closely tracks real vLLM serving behavior: TPOT and ITL stay within $4.8\%$ absolute error, E2E latency within $5.3\%$, and output throughput within $1.9\%$; TTFT is less stable, with maximum error $10.4\%$, reflecting its sensitivity to admission and queue state. These results suggest that lightweight, serving-native emulation can support practical online experimentation for LLM-serving systems. LLM-Emu is open sourced at https://github.com/AKafakA/llm-emu.

</details>


### [47] [Eliminating Hidden Serialization in Multi-Node Megakernel Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00686)
*Byungsoo Oh,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Perseus系统，通过解耦信令和NIC侧排序技术消除代理RDMA传输中的隐藏串行化，在多节点MoE推理上实现高达10.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大核MoE推理在单节点表现优异，但在多节点场景下性能严重退化（8节点退步达10倍），根源在于基于代理的RDMA传输中存在隐藏的串行化问题：每个tile传输与其完成信号之间的顺序约束强制设置栅栏，导致NIC流水线排空，且开销随并发传输数量增长。

Method: 提出两项关键技术：1）解耦信令（Decoupled signaling），按目标粒度批量处理栅栏，将栅栏数量减少8倍；2）NIC侧排序（NIC-side ordering），用硬件栅栏标志替代代理停滞，使代理永不阻塞。

Result: 在基于代理的传输上实现端到端最高10.3倍加速；在IBRC上Perseus匹配或超越IBGDA GPU-direct最高1.2倍，证明限制多节点大核性能的关键是串行化而非传输方式的选择。

Conclusion: 串行化而非代理传输与GPU直连传输的选择差异是多节点大核性能的根本瓶颈，Perseus通过消除串行化成功解决了该问题。

Abstract: Recent megakernel designs for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference fuse expert computation with fine-grained, GPU-initiated communication into a single persistent GPU kernel, and outperform collective-based MoE on a single node by overlapping data transfer with compute at tile granularity. This benefit does not carry over cleanly to multi-node inference, where experts span many nodes connected by an RDMA fabric. Communication-bound MoE models regress by up to $10\times$ on 8 nodes, and the regression worsens with node count. We trace this regression to hidden serialization in proxy-based RDMA transports. The ordering requirement between each tile transfer and its completion signal forces a fence that drains the NIC pipeline, and its cost grows with the number of concurrent transfers. As a result, models whose per-expert compute is too small to absorb this inflated network latency expose communication on the critical path. We present \emph{Perseus}, which eliminates this serialization through two techniques. \emph{Decoupled signaling} batches fences at per-destination granularity, reducing fence count by $8\times$. \emph{NIC-side ordering} replaces proxy stalls with hardware fence flags, so the proxy never blocks. On proxy-based transports, Perseus achieves up to 10.3$\times$ end-to-end speedup. Perseus on IBRC matches or exceeds IBGDA GPU-direct by up to 1.2$\times$, which shows that serialization, rather than the choice between proxy-based and GPU-direct transport, is what bounds multi-node megakernel performance.

</details>

### [48] [Cloud Is Closer Than It Appears: Revisiting the Tradeoffs of Distributed Real-Time Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00005)
*Pragya Sharma,Hang Qiu,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立形式化分析模型，证明了在具备高吞吐计算资源的条件下，云端推理能够有效抵消网络和排队延迟，在实时控制任务中表现优于设备端推理，挑战了云端推理不适用于延迟敏感任务的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在信息物理系统中的部署日益增加，传统分布式架构倾向于使用设备端推理以避免网络延迟，但这给本地硬件带来了巨大的能耗和计算压力。作者旨在重新审视“云端推理本质上不适合延迟敏感控制任务”这一假设。

Method: 开发了一个形式化分析模型，将分布式推理延迟表征为传感频率、平台吞吐量、网络延迟和任务特定安全约束的函数；以自动驾驶紧急制动为背景实例化该模型，并利用实时车辆动力学进行了广泛仿真验证。

Result: 实证结果确定了具体条件，在此条件下云端推理比设备端推理更可靠地遵守安全裕度，表明云端推理在满足实时性要求方面能够匹配或超越设备端性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了现有的设计策略，表明云端不仅是分布式CPS架构中可行的推理选项，而且在许多情况下是更优选的推理位置；云端实际上比传统认知中“更近”。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of deep neural networks (DNNs) in cyber-physical systems (CPS) enhances perception fidelity, but imposes substantial computational demands on execution platforms, posing challenges to real-time control deadlines. Traditional distributed CPS architectures typically favor on-device inference to avoid network variability and contention-induced delays on remote platforms. However, this design choice places significant energy and computational demands on the local hardware. In this work, we revisit the assumption that cloud-based inference is intrinsically unsuitable for latency-sensitive control tasks. We demonstrate that, when provisioned with high-throughput compute resources, cloud platforms can effectively amortize network and queueing delays, enabling them to match or surpass on-device performance for real-time decision-making. Specifically, we develop a formal analytical model that characterizes distributed inference latency as a function of the sensing frequency, platform throughput, network delay, and task-specific safety constraints. We instantiate this model in the context of emergency braking for autonomous driving and validate it through extensive simulations using real-time vehicular dynamics. Our empirical results identify concrete conditions under which cloud-based inference adheres to safety margins more reliably than its on-device counterpart. These findings challenge prevailing design strategies and suggest that the cloud is not merely a feasible option, but often the preferred inference location for distributed CPS architectures. In this light, the cloud is not as distant as traditionally perceived; in fact, it is closer than it appears.

</details>


### [49] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FedACT，一种面向多联邦学习任务的资源异构感知设备调度方法。通过设备与任务资源的对齐评分和公平参与机制，动态分配设备，显著降低了平均作业完成时间并提升了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习优化研究多集中于单一任务，而现实场景常需在共享设备池上同时训练多个模型。直接套用单任务优化技术会导致因设备异构性和资源利用低效而引起系统性能不佳，因此亟需针对多联邦学习任务的高效设备调度方案。

Method: 提出了FedACT调度方法。核心包含两点：1）基于对齐评分机制动态分配设备，评估设备可用资源与任务资源需求的兼容性；2）引入参与公平性约束，确保设备在各任务间贡献均衡。最终通过优先调度高对齐评分的设备并保证公平参与，生成最优调度计划。

Result: 在多种联邦学习任务和基准数据集上的实验表明，FedACT将平均作业完成时间（JCT）降低了最多8.3倍，并将模型精度提升了最多44.5%，显著优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: FedACT有效解决了多联邦学习系统中设备异构和资源效率低下的挑战，在降低JCT和提升模型精度方面均取得了显著成效。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [50] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个物理可解释性框架，包含两个互补的评估指标，用于验证数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律，发现低重建误差并不等同于物理一致性，且时间注意力机制对Transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型虽然能生成合理的微多普勒频谱图，但尚不清楚这些模型是否真正学习了底层的物理原理。研究者希望探究模型预测与物理规律的一致性，因为仅依靠重建误差无法全面评估模型的物理保真度。

Method: 提出了一个基于物理的可解释性框架，包含两个互补的评估指标：第一个指标测量模型预测与物理推导的多普勒频率之间的对齐程度；第二个指标通过速度干预测试预测是否保持速度-频率关系。这两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需实测雷达数据。

Result: 跨多个模型架构的实验表明，低重建误差并不能保证物理一致性。部分模型虽然实现了低误差，但在两个物理指标上表现不佳。进一步分析显示，时间注意力机制对于基于Transformer的模型学习底层物理规律至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在评估物理感知生成模型时，除了传统的重建误差指标外，还需要引入物理一致性评估。所提出的框架为验证数据驱动模型是否真正学习物理规律提供了有效工具，并为模型架构设计（特别是注意力机制）提供了指导。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [51] [High-Probability Convergence in Decentralized Stochastic Optimization with Gradient Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00281)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次为带梯度跟踪的去中心化随机梯度下降算法（GT-DSGD）建立了高概率收敛保证，在非凸和PL条件下达到了最优收敛率，且无需传统DSGD所需的严格假设（如数据异质性有界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化随机优化高概率收敛研究几乎只关注DSGD算法，该算法需要数据异质性有界或强凸性等强假设，与均方误差分析中已知偏差校正方法可在宽松假设下收敛且性能更优的结论形成反差。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 研究结合梯度跟踪技术（Gradient Tracking）的DSGD算法（GT-DSGD），在噪声满足宽松次高斯条件下，分析其高概率收敛性。针对非凸和Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) 两类目标函数进行分析。

Result: GT-DSGD在非凸和PL成本函数下分别达到了阶数为$\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{\sqrt{nT}}\Big)$和$\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{nT}\Big)$的最优高概率收敛率（$n$为智能体数量，$T$为时间范围，$δ$为置信参数）。该方法在成本函数条件要求上与MSE分析相同，同时保持了相当的瞬态时间。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对包含偏差校正的去中心化优化方法的高概率保证。结果表明GT-DSGD在放宽假设条件下依然保证高概率收敛，数值实验验证了GT-DSGD的优越性能，证明了偏差校正技术在高概率意义下的优势。

Abstract: We study high-probability (HP) convergence guarantees in decentralized stochastic optimization, where multiple agents collaborate to jointly train a model over a network. Existing HP results in decentralized settings almost exclusively focus on the Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\mathtt{DSGD}$) algorithm, which requires strong assumptions, such as bounded data heterogeneity, or strong convexity of each agent's cost. This is contrary to the mean-squared error (MSE) results, where methods incorporating bias-correction techniques are known to converge under relaxed assumptions and achieve better practical performance. In this paper we provide the first step toward bridging the gap, by studying HP convergence of $\mathtt{DSGD}$ incorporating the gradient tracking technique, in the presence of noise satisfying a relaxed sub-Gaussian condition. We show that the resulting method, dubbed $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$, achieves order-optimal HP convergence rates for both non-convex and Polyak-Łojasiewicz costs, of order $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{\sqrt{nT}}\Big)$ and $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{nT}\Big)$, respectively, where $n$ is the number of agents, $T$ is the time horizon and $δ\in (0,1)$ is the confidence parameter. Our results establish that $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ converges in the HP sense under the same conditions on the cost as in the MSE sense, while achieving comparable transient times. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first HP guarantees for decentralized optimization methods incorporating bias-correction. Numerical experiments on real and synthetic data verify our theoretical findings, underlining the superior performance of $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ and highlighting that the benefits of incorporating bias-correction are also maintained in the HP sense.

</details>


### [52] [AirFM-DDA: Air-Interface Foundation Model in the Delay-Doppler-Angle Domain for AI-Native 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00020)
*Kejia Bian,Meixia Tao,Jianhua Mo,Zhiyong Chen,Leyan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AirFM-DDA模型，通过将CSI从空时频域重参数化到时延-多普勒-角度(DDA)域解耦多径分量，结合窗口注意力机制与帧结构感知位置编码，实现了在物理层任务上的卓越零样本泛化能力，并大幅降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于空时频(STF)域信道状态信息(CSI)的基础模型存在多径分量叠加纠缠、难以学习通用信道表征的问题，且全局注意力机制计算开销过大，无法满足AI原生6G网络物理层设计的需求。

Method: 提出AirFM-DDA模型：1) 将CSI从STF域重参数化到时延-多普勒-角度(DDA)域，沿物理意义明确的轴显式解析多径分量；2) 采用窗口注意力模块替代全局注意力，降低计算复杂度并契合局部聚集的多径依赖关系；3) 引入帧结构感知位置编码(FS-PE)注入帧结构先验知识。

Result: 实验表明：1) 在信道预测与估计任务上，零样本泛化能力优于基线模型；2) 相比全局注意力，窗口注意力将训练与推理成本降低近一个数量级；3) 在高移动性、大时延扩展、强噪声及极端混叠条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AirFM-DDA通过DDA域表征与高效注意力机制，有效解决了现有模型多径分量纠缠与计算开销高的问题，为无线基础模型在6G物理层设计中的应用提供了高效、鲁棒且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Abstract: The success of large foundation models is catalyzing a new paradigm for AI-native 6G network design: wireless foundation models for physical layer design. However, existing models often operate on channel state information (CSI) in the space-time-frequency (STF) domain, where distinct multipath components are inherently superimposed and structurally entangled. This hinders the learning of universal channel representation. Meanwhile, their reliance on global attention mechanisms incurs prohibitive computational overhead. In this paper, we propose AirFM-DDA, an Air-interface Foundation Model operating in the Delay-Doppler-Angle (DDA) domain for physicallayer tasks. Specifically, AirFM-DDA reparameterizes CSI from the STF domain into the DDA domain to explicitly resolve multipath components along physically meaningful axes. It employs a window-based attention module augmented with framestructure-aware positional encoding (FS-PE). This window-based attention aligns with locally clustered multipath dependencies while avoiding quadratic-complexity global attention, and FS-PE injects frame-structure priors into network. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AirFM-DDA achieves superior zero-shot generalization across unseen scenarios and datasets, consistently outperforming the baselines on channel prediction and estimation tasks. Compared to the global attention, its window-based attention reduces training and inference costs by nearly an order of magnitude. Moreover, AirFM-DDA maintains robustness under high mobility, large delay spreads, severe noise, and extreme aliasing conditions.

</details>


### [53] [Meritocratic Fairness in Budgeted Combinatorial Multi-armed Bandits via Shapley Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00762)
*Shradha Sharma,Swapnil Dhamal,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对全乐队反馈的组合多臂老虎机问题，提出了基于K-Shapley值的公平性框架及K-SVFair-FBF算法，在保证公平性的同时实现了$O(T^{3/4})$的公平性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算约束的组合多臂老虎机问题中，传统方法主要关注半乐队反馈设置，而在全乐队反馈设置下，个体手臂的贡献无法直接观测，这使得实现精英主义公平性变得极具挑战性。作者旨在解决全乐队反馈场景下的公平性决策问题。

Method: 首先将合作博弈论中的Shapley值扩展为K-Shapley值，用于刻画受限规模集合中智能体的边际贡献，并证明其满足对称性、线性性、零玩家和效率性等性质。在此基础上，提出K-SVFair-FBF算法，该算法能够在估值函数未知的情况下自适应估计K-Shapley值，同时学习估值函数并缓解蒙特卡洛近似带来的噪声问题。

Result: 理论证明K-SVFair-FBF算法能够达到$O(T^{3/4})$的公平性遗憾界。在联邦学习和社会影响力最大化数据集上的实验表明，该方法在实现公平性的同时，比现有基线方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将Shapley值扩展到受限场景，并开发了适用于全乐队反馈设置的公平性感知算法，为组合决策问题中的公平性研究提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for meritocratic fairness in budgeted combinatorial multi-armed bandits with full-bandit feedback (BCMAB-FBF). Unlike semi-bandit feedback, the contribution of individual arms is not received in full-bandit feedback, making the setting significantly more challenging. To compute arm contributions in BCMAB-FBF, we first extend the Shapley value, a classical solution concept from cooperative game theory, to the $K$-Shapley value, which captures the marginal contribution of an agent restricted to a set of size at most $K$. We show that $K$-Shapley value is a unique solution concept that satisfies Symmetry, Linearity, Null player, and efficiency properties. We next propose K-SVFair-FBF, a fairness-aware bandit algorithm that adaptively estimates $K$-Shapley value with unknown valuation function. Unlike standard bandit literature on full bandit feedback, K-SVFair-FBF not only learns the valuation function under full feedback setting but also mitigates the noise arising from Monte Carlo approximations. Theoretically, we prove that K-SVFair-FBF achieves $O(T^{3/4})$ regret bound on fairness regret. Through experiments on federated learning and social influence maximization datasets, we demonstrate that our approach achieves fairness and performs more effectively than existing baselines.

</details>


### [54] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从公开交通事故报告重建事故场景的参数化多模态学习框架，并构建了包含6,217个真实案例的CISS-REC数据集，在事故点准确性和碰撞一致性方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故通常以文本报告形式记录，但物理上可验证的事故重建因缺乏详细的现场测量数据和专家重建而难以规模化进行，因为这类数据稀缺、成本高昂且难以扩展。因此，需要一种能够利用公开可获取的报告进行可扩展事故重建的方法。

Method: 将事故重建问题建模为参数化多模态学习问题。构建了CISS-REC数据集（包含6,217个来自NHTSA碰撞调查抽样系统的真实事故案例），并开发了一个重建框架。该框架将报告语义与道路拓扑和参与者属性进行对齐，重建车道一致性的碰撞前运动轨迹，并通过局部几何推理和时间分配优化碰撞相关的交互。

Result: 所提方法在CISS-REC数据集上优于代表性基线方法，实现了最佳的整体重建保真度，具体表现为更高的碰撞点准确性和碰撞一致性。

Conclusion: 研究表明公开的事故报告可以作为可扩展的计算基底，用于定量可验证的事故重建，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [55] [RunAgent: Interpreting Natural-Language Plans with Constraint-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00798)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RunAgent，一个多智能体计划执行平台，通过引入具有显式控制结构的智能体语言，弥合了自然语言表达力与编程确定性之间的差距，显著提升了LLM执行结构化工作流的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）能力强大，但在执行结构化工作流时仍缺乏可靠性。人类通过执行针对性计划来解决问题，而现有LLM难以兼顾自然语言的表达灵活性与程序执行的确定性。

Method: 提出RunAgent多智能体计划执行平台。核心方法包括：1) 设计一种包含显式控制结构（如IF、GOTO、FORALL）的智能体语言，连接自然语言与编程确定性；2) 除基于指令的步骤输出验证外，自主推导并验证基于任务描述的约束；3) 动态选择LLM推理、工具使用或代码生成执行；4) 引入错误纠正机制；5) 智能过滤上下文历史，仅保留相关信息。

Result: 在Natural-plan和SciBench数据集上的评估表明，RunAgent在性能上优于基线LLM和最先进的PlanGEN方法。

Conclusion: RunAgent成功地将自然语言计划的灵活性与结构化执行的确定性相结合，通过约束验证、动态执行策略选择及上下文管理，有效提升了对结构化工作流的执行可靠性。

Abstract: Humans solve problems by executing targeted plans, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for structured workflow execution. We propose RunAgent, a multi-agent plan execution platform that interprets natural-language plans while enforcing stepwise execution through constraints and rubrics. RunAgent bridges the expressiveness of natural language with the determinism of programming via an agentic language with explicit control constructs (e.g., \texttt{IF}, \texttt{GOTO}, \texttt{FORALL}). Beyond verifying syntactic and semantic verification of the step output, which is performed based on the specific instruction of each step, RunAgent autonomously derives and validates constraints based on the description of the task and its instance at each step. RunAgent also dynamically selects among LLM-based reasoning, tool usage, and code generation and execution (e.g., in Python), and incorporates error correction mechanisms to ensure correctness. Finally, RunAgent filters the context history by retaining only relevant information during the execution of each step. Evaluations on Natural-plan and SciBench Datasets demonstrate that RunAgent outperforms baseline LLMs and state-of-the-art PlanGEN methods.

</details>


### [56] [Smart Ensemble Learning Framework for Predicting Groundwater Heavy Metal Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00056)
*T. Ansah-Narh,G. Y. Afrifa,J. B. Tandoh,K. Asare,M. Addi,K. E. Yorke,D. M. A. Akpoley,K. Aidoo,S. K. Fosuhene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Densu盆地地下水重金属污染指数（HPI）的预测难题，提出了一种结合响应变换与集成机器学习的预测框架。发现高斯Copula变换效果最优，堆叠集成模型达到$R^2 = 0.96$，并结合DBSCAN聚类识别出Fe和Mn为主要污染贡献因子，为地下水污染评估提供了稳健且可解释的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Densu盆地的地下水正面临日益严重的重金属污染威胁。传统方法无法捕捉污染指标的统计复杂性和空间异质性，而重金属污染指数（HPI）通常呈现偏态分布且受相关污染物影响，导致未变换数据的预测存在偏差。因此需要开发能够处理这些统计特性的预测模型。

Method: 研究开发了一个整合响应变换与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习的预测框架。对HPI应用三种变换（原始、对数和高斯Copula），并在六种学习器上进行评估：支持向量回归（SVM）、k近邻（k-NN）、CART、Elastic Net、核岭回归以及堆叠Lasso集成。使用DBSCAN聚类分析识别主要污染贡献因子。

Result: 原始尺度模型产生了虚高的拟合度（Elastic Net和堆叠集成$R^2 \approx 1.0$），暗示过度乐观。对数变换稳定了方差（SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$）。高斯Copula变换结果最为可靠：堆叠集成$R^2 = 0.96$（RMSE $= 0.19$），其他学习器也保持高精度。基于Copula的模型改善了残差并生成了空间上合理的地图。DBSCAN聚类揭示Fe和Mn是HPI的主要贡献者。

Conclusion: 研究证明了分布感知的集成模型结合聚类诊断能够提供稳健、可解释的地下水污染评估。主要局限包括依赖随机（非空间）交叉验证和盆地特定范围。未来工作应探索空间验证和其他地质环境。该方法为地下水重金属污染预测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Groundwater in the Densu Basin is increasingly threatened by heavy metal contamination, but conventional methods fail to capture the statistical complexity and spatial heterogeneity of pollution indicators. A key challenge is modelling the Heavy Metal Pollution Index (HPI), which is typically skewed and affected by correlated contaminants, leading to biased predictions without transformation. This study develops a predictive framework integrating response transformations with nested cross-validated ensemble machine learning. Three transformations (raw, log, and Gaussian copula) were applied to HPI and evaluated across six learners: support vector regression (SVM), $k$-nearest neighbours (k-NN), CART, Elastic Net, kernel ridge regression, and a stacked Lasso ensemble. Raw-scale models produced deceptively high fits (Elastic Net and stacked ensemble $R^2 \approx 1.0$), suggesting over-optimism. The log transformation stabilised variance (SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$). The Gaussian copula gave the most reliable results: stacked ensemble $R^2 = 0.96$ (RMSE $= 0.19$), with other learners maintaining high accuracy. Copula-based models improved residuals and produced spatially plausible maps. DBSCAN clustering revealed Fe and Mn as primary HPI contributors, consistent with regional hydrogeochemistry. Limitations include reliance on random (not spatial) cross-validation and basin-specific scope. Future work should explore spatial validation and other geological settings. Overall, distribution-aware ensembles with clustering diagnostics offer robust, interpretable assessments of groundwater contamination.

</details>


### [57] [Information-Theoretic Generalization Bounds for Stochastic Gradient Descent with Predictable Virtual Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00064)
*Mohammad Partohaghighi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“可预测的历史自适应虚拟扰动”框架，突破了以往虚拟扰动分析中扰动协方差必须独立于优化历史的限制，为具有历史依赖性（如动量、预条件器）的SGD算法提供了新的信息论泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于虚拟扰动的SGD泛化界通常要求扰动协方差固定且独立于优化历史，这限制了其表示由移动梯度统计量、预条件器或曲率代理等引起的几何结构的能力。本文旨在突破这一限制，使虚拟扰动分析能够适应依赖于优化历史的SGD算法。

Method: 引入“可预测的历史自适应虚拟扰动”，允许每次迭代的扰动协方差依赖于过去的真实SGD历史，但不能依赖于当前或未来的随机性。利用可预测性进行条件高斯相对熵论证，并将局部高斯平滑与全局参考核比较分离，引入协方差比较成本项。

Result: 推导出了SGD的自适应泛化界，该界限用条件自适应对应项替代了固定的敏感度和梯度偏差项，包含了由累积扰动协方差引起的输出敏感度惩罚，并在条件无偏性下将偏差项简化为条件方差。界限还包含一个协方差比较成本，用于衡量使用可容许参考几何而非实际自适应协方差的KL散度代价。

Conclusion: 该框架将固定各向同性和几何感知界限作为特例恢复，并将虚拟扰动分析扩展到历史依赖的SGD，而无需修改算法本身。在可容许同步（如确定性、公开或前缀可观察协方差规则）下，可恢复固定噪声风格的界限。

Abstract: Information-theoretic generalization bounds analyze stochastic optimization by relating expected generalization error to the mutual information between learned parameters and training data. Virtual perturbation analyses of SGD add auxiliary Gaussian noise only in the proof, making mutual information tractable while leaving the actual SGD trajectory unchanged. Existing bounds, however, typically require perturbation covariances to be fixed independently of the optimization history, limiting their ability to represent geometries induced by moving gradient statistics, preconditioners, curvature proxies, and other pathwise information.
  We introduce predictable history-adaptive virtual perturbations, where the perturbation covariance at each iteration may depend on the past real SGD history but not on current or future randomness. This predictability enables a conditional Gaussian relative-entropy argument and yields generalization bounds for SGD with adaptive virtual-noise geometry. The bounds replace fixed sensitivity and gradient-deviation terms with conditional adaptive counterparts, include an output-sensitivity penalty from accumulated perturbation covariance, and reduce the deviation term to a conditional variance only under conditional unbiasedness.
  Since adaptive covariances may be data-dependent, we separate local Gaussian smoothing from global reference-kernel comparison. The resulting bound includes a covariance-comparison cost measuring the KL price of using an admissible reference geometry different from the actual adaptive covariance. Fixed-noise-style bounds are recovered under admissible synchronization, such as deterministic, public, or prefix-observable covariance rules. The framework recovers fixed isotropic and geometry-aware bounds as special cases while extending virtual perturbation analysis to history-dependent SGD without modifying the algorithm.

</details>


### [58] [Human-in-the-Loop Meta Bayesian Optimization for Fusion Energy and Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00068)
*Ricardo Luna Gutierrez,Sahand Ghorbanpour,Ejaz Rahman,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Riccardo Betti,Vineet Gundecha,Aarne Lees,Soumyendu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种人在回路元贝叶斯优化框架（HL-MBO），通过整合专家知识和少样本不确定性感知机器学习，在数据稀缺的高风险科学领域加速发现过程，在惯性约束聚变能量产额优化、分子优化和超导材料临界温度最大化任务上均优于现有贝叶斯优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性约束聚变（ICF）具有变革性清洁能源潜力，但面临实验成本极高、实验机会有限的挑战。在数据稀缺、高风险的科学领域，传统优化方法难以高效探索实验空间，亟需一种能够融合专家知识并加速发现的优化框架。

Method: 提出人在回路元贝叶斯优化（HL-MBO）框架：1）构建元学习代理模型，利用元学习从相关任务迁移知识以应对少样本场景；2）设计专家引导的采集函数，将领域专家知识融入实验推荐过程；3）提供可解释的推荐解释，增强用户信任并支持知情决策。该框架专门针对数据稀缺的高风险科学场景设计。

Result: HL-MBO在三个任务上验证有效：1）ICF能量产额优化任务中超越现有贝叶斯优化方法；2）分子优化基准测试中表现优异；3）超导材料临界温度最大化任务中取得更好性能。证明了该方法在数据稀缺科学领域具有普适性和优越性。

Conclusion: HL-MBO成功地将专家知识与不确定性感知机器学习相结合，为数据稀缺、高风险的科学发现领域提供了一种高效的优化范式。该框架不仅提升了优化性能，还通过可解释性增强了用户信任，为ICF等昂贵实验的科学探索提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) holds transformative promise for sustainable, near-limitless clean energy, yet remains constrained by prohibitively high costs and limited experimental opportunities. This paper presents Human-in-the-Loop Meta Bayesian Optimization (HL-MBO), a framework that integrates expert knowledge with few-shot, uncertainty-aware machine learning to accelerate discovery in data-scarce, high-stakes scientific domains. HL-MBO introduces a meta-learned surrogate model with an expert-informed acquisition function to recommend candidate experiments. To foster trust and enable informed decisions, HL-MBO also provides interpretable explanations of its suggestions. We show HL-MBO outperforms current BO methods on ICF energy yield optimization, as well as benchmarks in molecular optimization and critical temperature maximization for superconducting materials.

</details>


### [59] [Soft-MSM: Differentiable Context-Aware Elastic Alignment for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00069)
*Christopher Holder,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Soft-MSM，一种基于上下文感知转移成本的MSM平滑松弛方法，在112个UCR数据集上表现出比Soft-DTW更优的聚类和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Soft-DTW虽然可以通过梯度优化，但无法直接扩展到具有局部对齐上下文依赖的弹性距离（如MSM）。而MSM因其上下文感知的分裂和合并惩罚，在时间序列机器学习任务中常优于DTW，因此需要开发可微分的MSM变体。

Method: 提出Soft-MSM，核心是设计MSM分段分裂/合并成本的平滑门控替代函数，使梯度能够同时通过动态规划递归和局部转移结构进行传播。推导了前向递归、后向递归、软对齐矩阵、闭式梯度、极限行为和散度校正形式。

Result: 在112个UCR数据集上的实验表明，Soft-MSM比现有MSM重心方法产生更低的重心损失，在聚类和最近质心分类任务上显著优于基于Soft-DTW的替代方法。

Conclusion: Soft-MSM成功实现了MSM的可微分松弛，填补了上下文感知弹性距离在梯度优化方面的空白，为时间序列机器学习提供了更有效的损失函数，已集成到开源aeon工具包中。

Abstract: Elastic distances like dynamic time warping (DTW) are central to time series machine learning because they compare sequences under local temporal misalignment. Soft-DTW is an adaptation of DTW that can be used as a gradient-based loss by replacing the hard minimum in its dynamic-programming recursion with a smooth relaxation. However, this approach does not directly extend to elastic distances whose transition costs depend on the local alignment context. Move-Split-Merge (MSM) is one such distance: it uses context-aware split and merge penalties and has often outperformed DTW in supervised and unsupervised time series machine learning tasks such as classification and clustering.
  We introduce Soft-MSM, a smooth relaxation of MSM and an elastic alignment loss with context-aware transition costs. Central to the formulation is a smooth gated surrogate for MSM's piecewise split/merge cost, which enables gradients through both the dynamic-programming recursion and the local transition structure. We derive the forward recursion, backward recursion, soft alignment matrix, closed-form gradient, limiting behaviour, and divergence-corrected formulation. Experiments on 112 UCR datasets show that Soft-MSM gives lower MSM barycentre loss than existing MSM barycentre methods, and yields significantly better clustering and nearest-centroid classification performance than Soft-DTW-based alternatives. An implementation is available in the open-source \texttt{aeon} toolkit.

</details>


### [60] [CRADIPOR: Crash Dispersion Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00070)
*Edgar Chaillou,Sebastian Rodriguez,Yves Tourbier,Francisco Chinesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRADIPOR工具，通过基于秩降低自编码器（RRAE）和监督分类的方法，在不重复计算的情况下预测汽车碰撞仿真中的数值分散问题，为工程决策提供可靠依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车碰撞有限元模型在并行计算和模型复杂性影响下，仿真结果存在不可重复性，导致性能评估出现数值分散，而通过重复仿真评估分散的方法计算成本过高，不切实际。

Method: 提出一种结合秩降低自编码器（RRAE）和监督分类的预测框架，用于识别对数值分散敏感的区域。测试了多种信号表示方法（包括基于小波和基于斜率的输入），并与随机森林基线模型进行对比。

Result: RRAE框架在测试数据集上优于随机森林基线模型；在信号表示方法中，基于小波和基于斜率的输入效果最佳，其中斜率变化提供了最好的分类性能。

Conclusion: 结构化潜在表示能有效提升汽车碰撞后处理中数值分散检测的性能，支持将该预测工具应用于常规碰撞仿真后处理流程中。

Abstract: We present CRADIPOR, a numerical dispersion prediction tool for automotive crash simulations. Finite Element (FE) crash models are widely used throughout vehicle development, but their predictions are not strictly repeatable because of parallel computation and model complexity. As a result, performance criteria evaluated during post-processing may exhibit significant numerical dispersion, which complicates engineering decision-making. Although dispersion can be estimated by repeating the same simulation, this approach is generally impractical because of its high computational cost.
  This work therefore investigates a prediction tool that can be applied during routine crash-simulation post-processing without repeating the computation. The proposed approach relies on a Rank Reduction Autoencoder (RRAE) combined with supervised classification in order to identify regions sensitive to numerical dispersion. The comparative analysis suggests that the RRAE-based framework is more effective than the Random Forest baseline on the studied dataset. Among the tested signal representations, wavelet-based and slope-based inputs appear to be the most promising, with slope variations providing the best classification performance. These results support the use of structured latent representations for improving numerical-dispersion detection in automotive crash post-processing.

</details>


### [61] [Hyperspherical Forward-Forward with Prototypical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00082)
*Shalini Sarode,Brian Moser,Joachim Folz,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF)算法，通过将局部目标重构为超球面特征空间的多分类问题，解决了原FF算法推理计算成本高的问题，实现单次前向推理，速度提升超40倍，并在ImageNet-1k上取得超过25%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Forward-Forward (FF)算法虽然训练高效，但其推理过程需要对每个类别单独进行一次前向传播，计算成本极高，严重制约了其实用性。本文旨在解决这一关键瓶颈，在保持局部训练优势的同时实现高效推理。

Method: 提出Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF)方法，核心创新是将每层的局部目标从二元拟合任务重构为超球面特征空间内的直接多分类问题。通过学习一组类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，该方法可在单次前向传播中同时完成权重更新和推理。

Result: HFF推理速度比原始FF算法快40倍以上；在标准图像分类基准上准确率优于原FF并缩小了与反向传播的差距；成为首批在ImageNet-1k上报告超过25% top-1准确率（迁移学习达65.96%）的贪婪局部学习方法之一。

Conclusion: HFF成功解决了FF算法的推理瓶颈，兼具局部训练的生物学启发性与实际应用的高效性，为无需反向传播的深度学习提供了更具可扩展性和竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm presents a compelling, bio-inspired alternative to backpropagation. However, while efficient in training, it has a computationally prohibitive inference process that requires a separate forward pass for every class that is evaluated. In this work, we introduce the Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF), a novel reformulation that resolves this critical bottleneck. Our core innovation is to reframe the local objective of each layer from a binary goodness-of-fit task to a direct multi-class classification problem within a hyperspherical feature space. We achieve this by learning a set of class-specific, unit-norm prototypes that act as geometric anchors and implicit negatives. This architectural innovation preserves the benefits of local training while enabling weight update and inference in a single forward pass, making it >40x faster than the original FF algorithm. Our method is simple to implement, scales effectively to modern convolutional architectures, and achieves superior accuracy on standard image classification benchmarks, closing the gap with backpropagation. Most notably, we are among the first greedy local-learning methods to report over 25% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k, and 65.96% with transfer learning.

</details>


### [62] [Comparative Analysis of Polygon-Based and Global Machine Learning Models for Bus Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00083)
*Daniel Azenkot,Michael Fire,Eran Ben Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合空间聚类与多维特征分析的框架，通过将城市划分为不同区域并为每个区域训练独立的本地预测模型，实现了与全局模型相当的公交客流预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的公交客流预测模型通常将整个城市视为单一的同质区域，忽略了不同城市区域独特的局部动态特征，导致预测效果不佳。为了提高预测精度，需要一种能够捕捉局部区域特性的空间感知建模策略。

Method: 提出了一种结合空间聚类方法和多维特征分析的框架。首先整合公交客流数据（线路号、时间、站点）与多种开源数据（空间特征如吸引点、气象条件如温度降雨、时间模式如时段和星期几）。基于邻近公交站点具有相似客流特征的原则，对城市区域进行空间聚类，然后为每个聚类区域训练独立的本地预测模型。

Result: 这种本地化预测方法的准确性与全局模型相当，证明了空间感知的局部建模策略在公交客流预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 空间感知的局部建模策略能够有效提升公共交通预测的针对性和效率，为更有针对性的公共交通服务改进提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of bus ridership (passengers numbers) is crucial for efficient management and optimization of public transport systems. Traditional forecasting models often fail to capture the unique and localized dynamics of different urban areas by treating the entire city as a single, homogeneous region. This paper introduces a novel framework that enhances bus ridership prediction by integrating a spatial clustering methodology with multi-dimensional feature analysis. The proposed framework utilizes a diverse set of data, including bus ridership data (by route number, time, and bus stop) complemented by a variety of open source data, such as spatial features (e.g., attractive destinations), meteorological conditions (e.g., temperature, rainfall), and temporal patterns (e.g., time of day, day of week). By clustering the urban area into distinct regions, based on the principle that bus stops in close proximity share similar ridership characteristics, a separate local forecasting model is trained for each of these clusters. This localized approach demonstrates an accuracy comparable to that of global models. The findings suggest that a spatially-aware, localized modeling strategy is effective for public transport prediction, paving the way for more targeted and efficient service improvements.

</details>


### [63] [SPLICE: Latent Diffusion over JEPA Embeddings for Conformal Time-Series Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00126)
*Arnaud Zinflou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPLICE框架，将潜变量生成式填补与分布无关的自适应预测区间结合，在电力负荷填补任务中实现最优精度与93-95%的覆盖率保证，并具备跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列填补生成模型虽具强重构精度，但缺乏有限样本可靠性保证。在电系统中，填补值直接影响调度与规划决策，因此需要兼具高填补精度与不确定性量化能力的框架。

Method: SPLICE包含四个模块：(1) JEPA编码器将日负荷段映射至64维潜空间；(2) 条件潜变量桥接器以四种采样模式生成候选填补轨迹；(3) 小时条件解码器映射回信号空间；(4) 自适应共形推断(ACI)为输出包裹覆盖率有保证的预测带。采用流匹配变体可在5-10步ODE求解内达到DDIM质量。

Result: 在13个负荷数据集上：(1) 负荷专用MSE最低(0.056)，在91天缺口下赢得9/12非退化数据集，全部缺口长度下赢得18/32；(2) CRPS最优(0.161，较最强基线提升18.3%)；(3) ACI实现93-95%经验覆盖率，修正静态共形预测高达7.5个百分点的覆盖率不足；(4) 流匹配变体实现5-10倍加速；(5) 池化JEPA编码器迁移至4个未见域，经快速桥接微调即可匹敌或超越各数据集最优模型。

Conclusion: SPLICE成功将潜变量生成填补与分布无关的不确定性量化结合，在电力负荷填补任务中同时实现了最优重构精度、有效覆盖率保证、计算效率提升及跨域泛化能力，解决了生成式填补方法缺乏可靠性保证的关键局限。

Abstract: Generative models for time-series imputation achieve strong reconstruction accuracy, yet provide no finite-sample reliability guarantees, a critical limitation in power systems where imputed values inform dispatch and planning. We introduce SPLICE (Self-supervised Predictive Latent Inpainting with Conformal Envelopes), a modular framework coupling latent generative imputation with distribution-free, online-adaptive prediction intervals. A JEPA encoder maps daily load segments into a 64-dimensional latent space; a conditional latent bridge with four sampling modes generates candidate gap trajectories; an hourly-conditioned decoder maps back to signal space; and Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) wraps the output with coverage-guaranteed prediction bands. The flow-matching variant achieves comparable quality to DDIM in 5--10 ODE steps (5-10x speedup). On thirteen load datasets (nine proprietary, three UCI Electricity, ETTh1), SPLICE achieves the lowest mean Load-only MSE (0.056), winning 9/12 non-degenerate datasets at 91-day gaps and 18/32 across all gap lengths vs. five established baselines, and produces the best CRPS (0.161, -18.3% vs. the strongest competitor). ACI delivers 93--95% empirical coverage, correcting under-coverage failures of up to 7.5 pp observed with static conformal prediction. A pooled JEPA encoder trained on nine feeds transfers to four unseen domains, matching or exceeding per-dataset oracles with only a quick bridge fine-tuning.

</details>


### [64] [Learning Fingerprints for Medical Time Series with Redundancy-Constrained Information Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00130)
*Huayu Li,ZhengXiao He,Xiwen Chen,Jingjing Wang,Siyuan Tian,Jinghao Wen,Ao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自监督学习框架，通过交叉注意力瓶颈和双重目标函数将变长医疗时间序列压缩为k个解纠缠的指纹令牌，解决了现有方法无法学习紧凑且可解释潜在表示的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗时间序列（如ECG、EEG）具有高维、变长和噪声多的特点。现有的自监督方法（如MAE）虽然有效，但不能显式学习紧凑且语义可解释的潜在表示，通常依赖启发式聚合策略（如全局平均池化或[CLS]令牌），缺乏解纠缠能力。

Method: 提出一种新架构，使用交叉注意力瓶颈将变长MedTS压缩为固定大小的k个潜在指纹令牌。采用双重目标函数训练：1）重建损失，确保令牌是原始数据的充分统计量；2）基于总编码率（TCR）的多样性惩罚，显式最小化令牌间的冗余，鼓励统计解纠缠。该方法被构建为解纠缠率-失真问题。

Result: 该方法产生了低维、可解释且样本高效的表示，每个令牌被鼓励捕捉独立的变异因子，为更稳健的数字生物标志物铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 通过理论证明和实验验证，该框架成功学习到了解纠缠的、语义可解释的医疗时间序列表示，克服了现有方法的局限性，为医疗AI应用提供了更有效的特征提取方法。

Abstract: Learning meaningful representations from medical time series (MedTS) such as ECG or EEG signals is a critical challenge. These signals are often high-dimensional, variable-length and rife with noise. Existing self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Autoencoders (MAEs) are highly effective for pre-training general-purpose encoders. However, they do not explicitly learn compact and semantically interpretable latent representations, typically relying on heuristic aggregation strategies such as global average pooling or a designated [CLS] token. We propose a novel framework that compresses a variable-length MedTS into a fixed-size set of $k$ latent Fingerprint Tokens. Our architecture employs a cross-attention bottleneck to generate these tokens and is trained with a dual-objective function. The first objective is a reconstruction loss, which ensures the tokens are \textit{sufficient statistics} for the original data. The second, a diversity penalty based on the Total Coding Rate (TCR), explicitly minimizes the redundancy between tokens, encouraging them to become statistically \textit{disentangled} representations. We present the theoretical justification for our method, framing it as a novel \textbf{Disentangled Rate-Distortion} problem. This approach produces a low-dimensional, interpretable, and sample-efficient representation, where each token is encouraged to capture an independent factor of variation, paving the way for more robust digital biomarkers.

</details>


### [65] [Smart Profit-Aware Crop Advisory System: Kisan AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00133)
*Debasis Dwibedy,Avyay Nishtala,Pranathi Mukku,D Snehaja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了Kisan AI系统，通过在作物推荐模型中加入市场价格特征解决了现有系统的"经济盲区"问题，实现了99.3%的高预测准确率，并构建了多语言移动端全栈应用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物咨询系统存在"经济盲区"这一关键局限：系统主要优化生物产量，常忽视市场价格，导致农民做出农学合理但财务上不可行的决策。

Method: 在七个标准农学属性基础上增加市场价格变量，构建九特征基准数据集训练随机森林(RF)分类器，并与八个基线模型进行对比评估；将RF模型与Facebook Prophet六个月价格预测引擎、MobileNetV2病害检测模块集成，通过Anthropic Claude API构建九语言AI聊天机器人，开发多语言渐进式Web应用。

Result: RF模型在准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数和Log Loss等评估指标上表现最优，达到99.3%的最高准确率和最低Log Loss，验证了将市场价格作为预测特征的合理性和重要性。

Conclusion: Kisan AI成功构建了一个统一的全栈智能作物咨询平台，集成了利润感知推荐、价格预测和病害检测功能，通过多语言移动应用向印度农民提供可访问的服务。

Abstract: Modern crop advisory systems exhibit a critical limitation termed \textit{economic blindness}. These systems primarily optimize for biological yield, often overlooking market price, which can lead farmers toward agronomically sound yet financially unviable decisions. In this paper, we develop Kisan AI, a smart profit-aware crop advisory system that resolves the above-mentioned limitation through a research-driven, full-stack application. We train the Random Forest(RF) classifier model on a nine-feature benchmark dataset, the standard seven agronomic attributes augmented with a \textit{market\_price} variable, and evaluated against eight baseline models, considering the evaluation matrices, such as, accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Log Loss. The RF model achieves the highest accuracy of 99.3\% and the lowest Log Loss, confirming that the inclusion of market price as a predictive feature is both valid and impactful. We then implement the RF model within a multilingual progressive Web App alongside a Facebook Prophet six-month price forecasting engine and a MobileNetV2 disease detection module. A nine-language AI chatbot powered by the Anthropic Claude API unifies all modules into a single, mobile-installable platform accessible to farmers across India.

</details>


### [66] [Technical Report: Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ) for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00140)
*YiFeng Wang,Zhun Sun,Keisuke Sakaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARHQ（激活残差海森量化），一种后训练权重拆分方法，通过构建输入侧残差海森矩阵，将误差敏感的权重方向隔离到高精度低秩分支中，从而缓解低比特激活-权重量化中的误差传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低比特激活-权重量化中，量化误差会通过网络传播并累积，导致性能显著下降。现有方法难以有效识别和处理对误差敏感的权重方向，因此需要一种能够解析地识别并隔离这些敏感方向的方法，以缓解误差传播问题。

Method: ARHQ是一种后训练权重拆分方法。其核心步骤包括：(1) 利用激活量化残差(G_x)构建输入侧残差海森矩阵；(2) 对缩放权重矩阵W G^{1/2}_x执行闭式截断SVD，解析地识别误差敏感的权重方向；(3) 将这些敏感方向隔离到一个高精度低秩分支中，保持高精度计算。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507模型上的实验表明，ARHQ显著提升了层级信噪比(SNR)，并在激进的量化设置下，有效保留了ZebraLogic基准上的下游推理性能。

Conclusion: ARHQ通过解析地识别并隔离误差敏感的权重方向，成功缓解了低比特量化中的误差传播问题，为大语言模型的低比特量化提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ), a post-training weight splitting method designed to mitigate error propagation in low-bit activation-weight quantization. By constructing an input-side residual Hessian from activation quantization residuals (G_x), ARHQ analytically identifies and isolates error-sensitive weight directions into a high-precision low-rank branch. This is achieved via a closed-form truncated SVD on the scaled weight matrix W G^{1/2}_x . Experimental results on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 demonstrate that ARHQ significantly improves layer-wise SNR and preserves downstream reasoning performance on ZebraLogic even under aggressive quantization. The code is available at https://github.com/BeautMoonQ/ARHQ.

</details>


### [67] [Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Regret Optimization for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00155)
*Yikai Wang,Shang Liu,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein分布鲁棒遗憾优化(DRRO)方法，用于缓解RLHF中的奖励过度优化(Goodharting)问题，相比标准DRO方法更少悲观且更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中使用的奖励信号仅是真实人类效用的学习代理，导致目标误设问题：策略针对估计奖励优化，而部署性能由未观测目标决定。这种差距导致奖励过度优化(Goodharting)，即代理奖励持续改善而真实质量却在下降。现有缓解方法（不确定性惩罚、悲观奖励、保守约束）计算负担重且过度悲观。

Method: 提出Wasserstein分布鲁棒遗憾优化(DRRO)方法。不同于标准DRO对最坏情况价值的悲观化，DRRO对相同合理奖励扰动下相对于最优策略的最坏情况遗憾进行悲观化。通过单纯形分配模型研究提示级问题，在ℓ1模糊集下，内层最坏情况遗憾有精确解，最优策略具有注水结构。基于此开发了实用的策略梯度算法，具有简单的采样奖励解释，仅需对PPO/GRPO风格RLHF训练做微小修改。

Result: 理论分析表明DRRO比DRO更少悲观。实验结果显示，DRRO比现有基线更有效地缓解过度优化问题，而标准DRO则系统性过度悲观。

Conclusion: DRRO框架为RLHF中的目标误设问题提供了有效的解决方案，在理论上有力地解释了其相比标准DRO的优势，在实验上证明了其在缓解过度优化方面的有效性，同时保持了计算实用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has become a core post-training step for aligning large language models, yet the reward signal used in RLHF is only a learned proxy for true human utility. From an operations research perspective, this creates a decision problem under objective misspecification: the policy is optimized against an estimated reward, while deployment performance is determined by an unobserved objective. The resulting gap leads to reward over-optimization, or Goodharting, where proxy reward continues to improve even after true quality deteriorates. Existing mitigations address this problem through uncertainty penalties, pessimistic rewards, or conservative constraints, but they can be computationally burdensome and overly pessimistic. We propose Wasserstein distributionally robust regret optimization (DRRO) for RLHF. Instead of pessimizing worst-case value as in standard DRO, DRRO pessimizes worst-case regret relative to the best policy under the same plausible reward perturbation. We study the promptwise problem through a simplex allocation model and show that, under an $\ell_1$ ambiguity set, the inner worst-case regret admits an exact solution and the optimal policy has a water-filling structure. These results lead to a practical policy-gradient algorithm with a simple sampled-bonus interpretation and only minor changes to PPO/GRPO-style RLHF training. The framework also clarifies theoretically why DRRO is less pessimistic than DRO, and our experiments show that DRRO mitigates over-optimization more effectively than existing baselines while standard DRO is systematically over-pessimistic.

</details>


### [68] [Consistent Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00161)
*Hasan Amin,Yuan Gao,Yaser Souri,Subhojit Som,Ming Yin,Rajiv Khanna,Xia Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDLM（一致扩散语言模型），一种基于多路径离散一致性（MPDC）原则的单阶段、无教师训练框架，显著加速了离散扩散模型的采样过程，在文本生成任务上取得了新的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）虽然理论上能够实现亚线性时间的并行生成，但实际应用中高质量样本仍需数百次精炼步骤，限制了其实用性。连续域中的一致性训练可以通过概率流ODE加速扩散，但离散扩散缺乏类似的样本空间ODE，使得直接适配无法定义，因此需要为离散扩散寻找有效的加速方法。

Method: 提出多路径离散一致性（MPDC）原则，将精确的后验桥作为离散域中ODE的自然替代。通过训练去噪器在随机桥的期望上保持路径不变性，开发了CDLM框架。该框架是单阶段、无教师的训练方法，统一了掩码扩散、连续一致性模型以及渐进/离散蒸馏等方法。

Result: CDLM在条件和无条件文本生成任务上均建立了新的最先进性能，一致性地超越强基线离散扩散模型，在多种采样预算下（尤其在少步采样场景）甚至优于多阶段蒸馏的基线模型。

Conclusion: CDLM为下一代快速、高保真离散生成建模提供了有原则且可扩展的基础，有效解决了离散扩散模型采样效率低的问题，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) are an attractive alternative to autoregressive models because they promise sublinear-time, parallel generation, yet practical gains remain elusive as high-quality samples still demand hundreds of refinement steps. In continuous domains, consistency training along the probability-flow ODE is a popular recipe to accelerate diffusion. For discrete diffusion, no analogous sample-space ODE exists, making direct adaptation ill-defined. We argue that the natural discrete substitute is not a deterministic trajectory but its stochastic counterpart: the exact posterior bridge, available in closed form for broad corruption families including masked and uniform diffusion. Building on this observation, we introduce Multi-Path Discrete Consistency (MPDC), a new principle that trains a denoiser to be path-invariant in expectation across these stochastic bridges, and instantiate it as the Consistent Diffusion Language Model (CDLM), a single-stage, teacher-free training framework. A single CDLM objective unifies masked diffusion, continuous consistency models, and progressive/discrete distillation as analytic limits or empirical approximations of one common view. Empirically, CDLM establishes a new state of the art on both conditional and unconditional text-generation, consistently outperforming strong base discrete diffusion models and often even multi-stage distilled baselines across sampling budgets, with the largest gains in the few-step regime.
